;const img=await v1102LoadImage(file),ow=img.naturalWidth||img.width,oh=img.naturalHeight||img.height
;if(!ow||!oh)throw new Error("写真の大きさを確認できませんでした。");let scale=Math.min(1,1280/Math.max(ow,oh)),quality=0.82,blob=null;for(let n=0;n<8;n++){

;canvas.getContext("2d").drawImage(img,0,0,w,h);blob=await new Promise(r=>canvas.toBlob(r,"image/jpeg",quality));if(blob&&blob.size<=512000)return{

;return Number.isFinite(n)?n:NaN}function v1115LotUnitWeight(lot){

/* ===== v1.119.1 本番安全化: 棚卸し台帳・QR誤登録訂正 ===== */
const V119_APP_VERSION="v1.119";
window.APP_VERSION=V119_APP_VERSION;
document.title="SEIKA＋ v1.119 棚卸し・QR訂正版";

function v119CanPriceInput(){return ["admin","sales","clerk"].includes(db.role)}
function v119CanCountView(){return ["admin","worker","sales","clerk"].includes(db.role)}
function v119StrictDecimal(value){
  let raw=String(value??"").trim();
  if(!raw||raw.includes("、"))return null;
  raw=raw.replace(/[０-９]/g,c=>String.fromCharCode(c.charCodeAt(0)-0xfee0)).replace(/．/g,".").replace(/，/g,",");
  if(!/^(?:(?:\d{1,3}(?:,\d{3})+|\d+)(?:\.\d+)?|\.\d+)$/.test(raw))return null;
  const n=Number(raw.replace(/,/g,""));return Number.isFinite(n)?n:null;
}
function v119Num(value){return typeof value==="number"&&Number.isFinite(value)?value:v119StrictDecimal(value)}
function v119RoleLabel(){return({admin:"管理者",worker:"青果社員",sales:"営業",clerk:"事務"}[db.role]||ROLE?.[db.role]||db.role||"-")}
function v119IsKgLine(line){return line?.type==="normal"||String(line?.unit||"").toLowerCase()==="kg"}
function v119SingleKg(value){
  const raw=String(value??"").trim().replace(/[０-９]/g,c=>String.fromCharCode(c.charCodeAt(0)-0xfee0)).replace(/．/g,".");
  const match=raw.match(/^(\d+(?:\.\d+)?|\.\d+)\s*kg$/i);if(!match)return null;
  const n=Number(match[1]);return Number.isFinite(n)&&n>0?yen(n):null;
}
function v119EnsureCountLine(line){
  if(!line)return line;
  if(!["detail","total"].includes(line.detailMode))line.detailMode="total";
  if(!v119IsKgLine(line))line.detailMode="total";
  if(line.packKg===undefined||line.packKg===null)line.packKg="";
  if(line.caseCount===undefined||line.caseCount===null)line.caseCount="";
  if(line.remainderKg===undefined||line.remainderKg===null)line.remainderKg="";
  if(line.kgPerUnit===undefined||line.kgPerUnit===null)line.kgPerUnit="";
  if(line.supplier===undefined||line.supplier===null)line.supplier="";
  line.supplierRequired=Boolean(line.supplierRequired);return line;
}
function v119EnsureCountSession(session){
  if(!session)return session;(session.lines||[]).forEach(v119EnsureCountLine);
  session.priceStatus=session.priceStatus||((session.status==="confirmed"||session.status==="reversed")?"waiting_price":"");return session;
}
function v119EnsureAllCountSessions(){countSessions().forEach(v119EnsureCountSession)}
function v119CountStatus(session){
  if(session?.detailsDeletedAt)return"詳細削除済み";
  if(session?.status==="confirmed"&&session.priceStatus==="waiting_price")return"単価入力待ち";
  if(session?.status==="confirmed"&&session.priceStatus==="priced")return"単価入力済み";
  return countStatusLabel(session);
}
function v119SaveSessionLocal(session,action,detail=""){
  session.updatedAt=nowIso();session.updatedBy=db.user;session.revision=Number(session.revision||0)+1;if(action)countLog(action,session,detail);save();
}
function v119LineActualKg(line,isQr=false){
  if(!line)return null;const actual=v119Num(line.actualQty);if(actual===null||actual<0)return null;
  if(!isQr&&v119IsKgLine(line))return yen(actual);
  if(isQr&&qrIsLargeContainer(line.containerType)){
    const weight=v119Num(qrCountActualWeight(line));return weight===null||weight<0?null:yen(weight);
  }
  const explicit=v119StrictDecimal(line.kgPerUnit);
  if(explicit!==null&&explicit>0)return yen(actual*explicit);
  const safe=isQr?v119SingleKg(line.packWeight):null;
  return safe===null?null:yen(actual*safe);
}
function v119LinePriceAmount(line,isQr=false){const kg=v119LineActualKg(line,isQr),price=v119StrictDecimal(line.unitPriceYenPerKg);return kg===null||price===null||price<0?null:Math.round(kg*price)}
function v119AllPriceRows(session){return[...(session?.lines||[]).map(line=>({line,isQr:false})),...(session?.qrLines||[]).map(line=>({line,isQr:true}))]}
function v119PricesComplete(session){const rows=v119AllPriceRows(session);return rows.length>0&&rows.every(({line,isQr})=>{const kg=v119LineActualKg(line,isQr),price=v119StrictDecimal(line.unitPriceYenPerKg);return kg!==null&&kg>0&&price!==null&&price>=0})}
function v119CalendarMonthsPassed(iso,months=3){if(!iso)return false;const from=new Date(iso),today=new Date();if(Number.isNaN(from.getTime()))return false;const due=new Date(from);due.setMonth(due.getMonth()+months);return today>=due}
function v119CanDeleteCountDetails(session){return Boolean(canAdmin()&&session&&session.status==="confirmed"&&!session.reversedAt&&session.documentSavedAt&&!session.detailsDeletedAt&&v119CalendarMonthsPassed(session.confirmedAt,3))}

function v119SetCountDetailMode(sessionId,lineId,mode){

function v119CountInputValue(sessionId,lineId){
  const session=countSession(sessionId),line=session?.lines?.find(row=>row.lineId===lineId);
  if(!session||!line||!countEditable(session))return;v119EnsureCountLine(line);
  const isKg=v119IsKgLine(line),totalInput=document.getElementById("count_"+lineId),memoInput=document.getElementById("countmemo_"+lineId),supplierInput=document.getElementById("countsupplier_"+lineId),requiredInput=document.getElementById("countsupplierrequired_"+lineId);
  if(isKg&&line.detailMode==="detail"){
    const packRaw=document.getElementById("countpack_"+lineId)?.value??"",caseRaw=document.getElementById("countcase_"+lineId)?.value??"",remainderRaw=document.getElementById("countremainder_"+lineId)?.value??"";
    if(!String(packRaw).trim()&&!String(caseRaw).trim()&&!String(remainderRaw).trim()){
      line.actualQty=null;line.status="unentered";line.packKg="";line.caseCount="";line.remainderKg="";
    }else{
      const pack=v119StrictDecimal(packRaw),cases=v119StrictDecimal(caseRaw),remainder=String(remainderRaw).trim()===""?0:v119StrictDecimal(remainderRaw);
      if(pack===null||pack<=0)return v161FocusField("countpack_"+lineId,"量目kgは0より大きい数値で入力してください。");
      if(cases===null||cases<0)return v161FocusField("countcase_"+lineId,"ケース数は0以上で入力してください。");
      if(remainder===null||remainder<0)return v161FocusField("countremainder_"+lineId,"端数kgは0以上で入力してください。");
      line.packKg=yen(pack);line.caseCount=yen(cases);line.remainderKg=yen(remainder);line.actualQty=yen(pack*cases+remainder);line.status="entered";
    }
  }else{
    const raw=String(totalInput?.value??"").trim();
    if(!raw){line.actualQty=null;line.status="unentered"}else{
      const value=v119StrictDecimal(raw);if(value===null||value<0)return v161FocusField("count_"+lineId,`${line.itemName}：実数は0以上の数値で入力してください。`);
      line.actualQty=yen(value);line.status="entered";
    }
    if(!isKg&&line.actualQty!==null){
      const kgPerUnit=v119StrictDecimal(document.getElementById("countkgperunit_"+lineId)?.value);
      if(kgPerUnit===null||kgPerUnit<=0)return v161FocusField("countkgperunit_"+lineId,`1${line.unit||"単位"}あたりkgを0より大きい数値で入力してください。`);
      line.kgPerUnit=yen(kgPerUnit);
    }
  }
  line.supplier=supplierInput?.value.trim()||"";line.supplierRequired=Boolean(requiredInput?.checked);
  if(line.supplierRequired&&!line.supplier)return v161FocusField("countsupplier_"+lineId,"この品目は仕入先が必要です。入力してください。");
  line.memo=memoInput?.value.trim()||"";if(line.actualQty!==null){line.countedAt=nowIso();line.countedBy=db.user;line.countedById=db.userId}
  const unit=isKg?"kg":line.unit||"";v119SaveSessionLocal(session,"実数入力",`${line.itemName}: ${line.actualQty===null?"未入力":fmt(line.actualQty)+unit}`);inventoryCountPage(sessionId);
}

  const status=!entered?"":diff===0?"done":diff<0?"diffNeg":"diffPos",badge=!entered?"未入力":diff===0?"差異なし":(diff>0?"+":"")+fmt(diff)+" "+unit;
  const supplier=editable?`<div class="row" style="margin-top:8px"><div><label class="label">仕入先 ${line.supplierRequired?'<span style="color:#dc2626">必須</span>':'（必要時）'}</label><input id="countsupplier_${esc(line.lineId)}" class="input" value="${esc(line.supplier||"")}" placeholder="必要な品目のみ入力"></div><div><label class="label">仕入先を必須にする</label><label class="checkRow"><input id="countsupplierrequired_${esc(line.lineId)}" type="checkbox" ${line.supplierRequired?"checked":""}> この品目は仕入先確認が必要</label></div></div>`:`${line.supplier?`<div class="small" style="margin-top:7px">仕入先：${esc(line.supplier)}${line.supplierRequired?"（確認必須）":""}</div>`:""}`;
  const mode=editable&&isKg?`<div class="countFilter" style="margin-top:9px"><button class="btn btn2 ${!detail?"on":""}" onclick="v119SetCountDetailMode('${esc(session.id)}','${esc(line.lineId)}','total')">合計kg入力</button><button class="btn btn2 ${detail?"on":""}" onclick="v119SetCountDetailMode('${esc(session.id)}','${esc(line.lineId)}','detail')">量目kg×ケース＋端数kg</button></div>`:"";
  const qty=detail?`<div class="row" style="margin-top:9px"><div><label class="label">量目kg</label><input id="countpack_${esc(line.lineId)}" class="input" inputmode="decimal" value="${esc(line.packKg??"")}" placeholder="例：10"></div><div><label class="label">ケース数</label><input id="countcase_${esc(line.lineId)}" class="input" inputmode="decimal" value="${esc(line.caseCount??"")}" placeholder="例：20"></div><div><label class="label">端数kg</label><input id="countremainder_${esc(line.lineId)}" class="input" inputmode="decimal" value="${esc(line.remainderKg??"")}" placeholder="0"></div></div><div class="small" style="margin-top:6px">合計実数：<b>${entered?fmt(line.actualQty)+" kg":"未入力"}</b></div>`:`<div class="row" style="margin-top:9px"><div><label class="label">合計実数（${esc(unit)}）</label><input id="count_${esc(line.lineId)}" class="input countQty" inputmode="decimal" value="${entered?esc(line.actualQty):""}" placeholder="空欄=未入力 / 0=実数ゼロ" ${editable?"":"disabled"}></div>${!isKg?`<div><label class="label">1${esc(unit)}あたりkg <span style="color:#dc2626">必須</span></label><input id="countkgperunit_${esc(line.lineId)}" class="input" inputmode="decimal" value="${esc(line.kgPerUnit??"")}" placeholder="例：10" ${editable?"":"disabled"}></div>`:""}<div><label class="label">メモ</label><input id="countmemo_${esc(line.lineId)}" class="input" value="${esc(line.memo||"")}" ${editable?"":"disabled"}></div></div>`;
  const detailMemo=detail?`<label class="label" style="margin-top:8px">メモ</label><input id="countmemo_${esc(line.lineId)}" class="input" value="${esc(line.memo||"")}">`:"";
  return`<section class="card countLine ${status}"><div class="countMeta"><div><b>${esc(line.itemName)}</b><div class="small">${esc(line.group)} / 開始時帳簿：${fmt(line.systemQty)} ${esc(unit)}</div></div><span class="badge ${!entered?"unset":diff===0?"normal":diff<0?"shortage":"excess"}">${badge}</span></div>${mode}${qty}${detailMemo}${supplier}${editable?`<button class="btn green" style="width:100%;margin-top:10px" onclick="v119CountInputValue('${esc(session.id)}','${esc(line.lineId)}')">この品目を保存</button>`:""}</section>`;
}
function inventoryCountPage(id,msg=""){
  setScreen("inventoryCountPage",[id,msg]);const session=countSession(id);if(!session)return inventoryCountHome("棚卸しデータが見つかりません。");v119EnsureCountSession(session);
  const stats=countStats(session),qrStats=qrCountStats(session),editable=countEditable(session),gone=Boolean(session.detailsDeletedAt),normal=(gone?[]:session.lines||[]).map(line=>v119CountLineHtml(session,line,editable)).join("")||'<div class="small">明細は削除済みです。</div>',qr=(gone?[]:session.qrLines||[]).map(line=>qrCountLineHtml(session,line,editable)).join("")||'<div class="small">明細は削除済みです。</div>';
  const submit=editable?`<button class="btn green" onclick="submitInventoryCount('${esc(session.id)}')">入力完了として提出</button><button class="btn red" onclick="cancelInventoryCount('${esc(session.id)}')">棚卸しを破棄</button>`:"",review=session.status==="submitted"&&canAdmin()?`<button class="btn orange" style="width:100%;margin-top:10px" onclick="inventoryCountReviewPage('${esc(session.id)}')">管理者確認へ進む</button>`:"",price=session.status==="confirmed"&&v119CanPriceInput()?`<button class="btn blue" style="width:100%;margin-top:10px" onclick="v119InventoryCountPricePage('${esc(session.id)}')">${session.priceStatus==="priced"?"単価を確認・修正":"単価を入力"}</button>`:"";
  pageShell("棚卸し入力",`${esc(session.countNo)} / ${esc(session.roomLabel)} / ${esc(v119CountStatus(session))}`,`${okHtml(msg)}${gone?'<div class="card notice"><b>保存済み文書に基づき、明細は削除済みです。</b></div>':`<div class="card"><div class="countStatGrid"><div class="countStat"><span class="small">通常対象</span><b>${stats.total}</b></div><div class="countStat"><span class="small">通常未入力</span><b>${stats.missing}</b></div><div class="countStat"><span class="small">QR未確認</span><b>${qrStats.missing}</b></div><div class="countStat"><span class="small">QR差異</span><b>${qrStats.diff}</b></div></div></div><div class="card"><b>通常・その他在庫</b><div class="small">kg管理行だけ「量目kg×ケース＋端数kg」を使えます。その他在庫は固有単位と1単位あたりkgを記録します。</div>${normal}</div><div class="card notice"><b>QRロット棚卸し（${qrStats.entered}/${qrStats.total}確認済）</b>${editable?`<div class="grid" style="margin-top:10px"><button class="btn blue" onclick="qrCountOpenCamera('${esc(session.id)}')">QRを連続読取</button><div><input id="qrCountManualKey" class="input" placeholder="QRキー / ロット番号"><button class="btn btn2" style="width:100%;margin-top:6px" onclick="qrCountManualAdd('${esc(session.id)}')">手動検索・追加</button></div></div>`:""}${qr}</div><div class="card"><div class="grid">${submit}<button class="btn btn2" onclick="printInventoryCount('${esc(session.id)}','sheet')">記入票を印刷</button>${session.status!=="counting"?`<button class="btn btn2" onclick="printInventoryCount('${esc(session.id)}','diff')">差異表を印刷</button>`:""}</div>${review}</div>`}${session.status==="confirmed"?`<div class="card ${session.priceStatus==="priced"?"ok":"notice"}"><b>${session.priceStatus==="priced"?"単価入力済み":"単価入力待ち"}</b><div class="small">単価・Excel・文書保存確認はサーバー側台帳で記録します。</div>${price}</div>`:""}`);
  document.querySelectorAll(".qrCountSave").forEach(button=>button.addEventListener("click",()=>qrCountInputFromButton(button)));
}
const v119ConfirmInventoryCountCore=confirmInventoryCount;

function v119InventoryCountPricePage(id,msg=""){
  setScreen("v119InventoryCountPricePage",[id,msg]);const session=countSession(id);if(!session||session.status!=="confirmed"||session.reversedAt||session.detailsDeletedAt)return inventoryCountHome("単価入力できる棚卸しではありません。");if(!v119CanPriceInput())return inventoryCountHome("単価を入力できるのは管理者・営業・事務です。");
  const rows=v119AllPriceRows(session),row=({line,isQr},index)=>{const kg=v119LineActualKg(line,isQr),amount=v119LinePriceAmount(line,isQr),label=isQr?`QR / ${line.lotNo||line.qrKey||"-"}`:line.type==="special"?"その他":"通常";return`<tr><td>${esc(label)}</td><td><b>${esc(line.itemName||"-")}</b></td><td>${esc(isQr?(line.containerType||line.unit||"-"):(line.unit||"kg"))}</td><td>${kg===null?'<span style="color:#b45309">kg換算不可</span>':fmt(kg)}</td><td><input id="v119price_${index}" class="input" inputmode="decimal" value="${esc(line.unitPriceYenPerKg??"")}" placeholder="円/kg"></td><td>${amount===null?"－":fmt(amount)+" 円"}</td></tr>`};
  pageShell("棚卸し単価入力",`${esc(session.countNo)} / 入力者：${esc(v119RoleLabel())}`,`${okHtml(msg)}<div class="card notice"><b>空欄単価は保存できません。</b><div class="small">全行で0より大きいkg換算値と0以上の単価が必要です。実数ゼロの行がある場合は単価確定できません。数量は変更されません。</div></div><div class="card"><div class="weeklyWrap"><table class="table"><thead><tr><th>区分</th><th>品名・ロット</th><th>荷姿</th><th>棚卸kg</th><th>単価（円/kg）</th><th>金額</th></tr></thead><tbody>${rows.map(row).join("")}</tbody></table></div><button class="btn green" style="width:100%;margin-top:14px" onclick="v119SaveInventoryCountPrices('${esc(session.id)}')">単価を保存</button></div>`);
}
async function v119SaveInventoryCountPrices(id){
  const session=countSession(id);if(!session||!v119CanPriceInput()||v161PendingCount())return alert("未同期データを保存してから単価を入力してください。");const rows=v119AllPriceRows(session),prices=[],invalid=[];
  rows.forEach(({line,isQr},index)=>{const price=v119StrictDecimal(document.getElementById("v119price_"+index)?.value),kg=v119LineActualKg(line,isQr);if(kg===null||kg<=0)invalid.push(`${line.itemName||"品名"}：0より大きいkg換算値が必要です（実数ゼロの行は単価確定できません）`);if(price===null||price<0)invalid.push(`${line.itemName||"品名"}：単価は空欄不可・0以上です`);prices.push({lineId:isQr?null:String(line.lineId||""),qrKey:isQr?String(line.qrKey||line.qr_key||""):null,unitPriceYenPerKg:price})});
  if(invalid.length)return alert(invalid.slice(0,4).join("\n"));if(!confirm("全行の単価と金額をサーバーへ保存します。棚卸し数量は変更されません。よろしいですか？"))return;
  v161ShowProcessing("単価を安全に保存しています");try{const ans=await callRpc("v119_save_inventory_count_prices",{p_id:STATE_ID,p_expected_version:stateVersion,p_session_id:id,p_prices:prices});if(ans?.conflict)return alert(ans.message||"他端末で更新されています。最新データを読み込んでください。");if(!ans?.ok)throw new Error(ans?.message||"単価を保存できませんでした");await loadCloud();inventoryCountHome("単価を保存しました。Excelを出力してください。")}catch(error){alert(`単価を保存できませんでした。\n${v161ErrorText(error)}`)}finally{v161HideProcessing()}
}
function v119InventoryCountExcelRows(session){return v119AllPriceRows(session).map(({line,isQr})=>({group:isQr?"QR":line.type==="special"?"その他":"通常",item:line.itemName||"",lot:isQr?(line.lotNo||line.qrKey||""):"",supplier:line.supplier||line.supplier_name||"",container:isQr?(line.containerType||line.unit||""):(line.unit||"kg"),packKg:isQr?(line.packWeight||""):(line.packKg??""),cases:line.caseCount??"",remainderKg:line.remainderKg??"",actualKg:v119LineActualKg(line,isQr),systemQty:line.systemQty??"",actualQty:line.actualQty??"",diff:line.actualQty===null||line.actualQty===undefined?"":yen(Number(line.actualQty)-Number(line.systemQty||0)),unitPrice:v119StrictDecimal(line.unitPriceYenPerKg),amount:v119LinePriceAmount(line,isQr),memo:line.memo||""}))}
downloadInventoryCountExcel=async function(id){
  const session=countSession(id);if(!session||session.status!=="confirmed"||session.reversedAt||session.priceStatus!=="priced"||!v119PricesComplete(session))return alert("単価入力済みで全行のkg換算が完了した棚卸しだけ出力できます。");if(!ensureXlsxReady())return;
  const rows=v119InventoryCountExcelRows(session),heads=["区分","品名","ロット","仕入先","荷姿","量目kg","ケース数","端数kg","棚卸kg","開始時帳簿","棚卸し実数","差異","単価（円/kg）","金額（円）","メモ"],meta=[["棚卸し番号",session.countNo],["状態","単価入力済み"],["冷蔵庫",session.roomLabel],["開始者",session.startedBy||""],["確定者",session.confirmedBy||""],["確定日時",session.confirmedAt?dt(session.confirmedAt):""],["単価入力者",session.pricedBy||""]],value=row=>[row.group,row.item,row.lot,row.supplier,row.container,row.packKg,row.cases,row.remainderKg,row.actualKg,row.systemQty,row.actualQty,row.diff,row.unitPrice,row.amount,row.memo].map(inventoryCountExcelSafeText),aoa=[[`棚卸し結果（単価・金額入り） ${session.roomLabel} ${session.countNo}`],[],...meta,[],heads,...rows.map(value)],ws=XLSX.utils.aoa_to_sheet(aoa),headerRow=meta.length+3,lastRow=headerRow+rows.length;
  ws["!merges"]=[{s:{r:0,c:0},e:{r:0,c:heads.length-1}}];ws["!autofilter"]={ref:XLSX.utils.encode_range({s:{r:headerRow,c:0},e:{r:lastRow,c:heads.length-1}})};ws["!freeze"]={xSplit:0,ySplit:headerRow+1};ws["!cols"]=[10,22,18,18,15,10,10,10,12,13,13,11,14,14,36].map(wch=>({wch}));
  for(let c=0;c<heads.length;c++)inventoryCountExcelStyle(ws,XLSX.utils.encode_cell({r:headerRow,c}),{font:{name:"Yu Gothic",sz:10,bold:true,color:{rgb:"102A43"}},alignment:{horizontal:"center",vertical:"center",wrapText:true},fill:{patternType:"solid",fgColor:{rgb:"D9EAF7"}},border:{top:{style:"thin"},bottom:{style:"thin"},left:{style:"thin"},right:{style:"thin"}}});
  for(let r=headerRow+1;r<=lastRow;r++)for(let c=0;c<heads.length;c++)inventoryCountExcelStyle(ws,XLSX.utils.encode_cell({r,c}),{font:{name:"Yu Gothic",sz:10},alignment:{horizontal:[5,6,7,8,9,10,11,12,13].includes(c)?"right":"left",vertical:"center",wrapText:true},border:{top:{style:"thin"},bottom:{style:"thin"},left:{style:"thin"},right:{style:"thin"}}});
  inventoryCountExcelStyle(ws,"A1",{font:{name:"Yu Gothic",sz:15,bold:true,color:{rgb:"FFFFFF"}},alignment:{horizontal:"center"},fill:{patternType:"solid",fgColor:{rgb:"1E3A5F"}}});const wb=XLSX.utils.book_new();XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb,ws,safeSheetName(session.roomLabel||"棚卸し"));const file=`棚卸し結果_単価金額入り_${String(session.roomLabel||"冷蔵庫").replace(/[\\/:*?"<>|]/g,"_")}_${String(session.countNo||"棚卸し").replace(/[\\/:*?"<>|]/g,"_")}.xlsx`;downloadWorkbookXlsx(wb,file);
  try{const ans=await callRpc("v119_mark_inventory_count_excel_export",{p_id:STATE_ID,p_expected_version:stateVersion,p_session_id:id,p_file_name:file});if(ans?.conflict)return alert(ans.message||"他端末で更新されています。最新データを読み込んでください。");if(!ans?.ok)throw new Error(ans?.message||"Excel出力記録に失敗しました");await loadCloud();inventoryCountHome("金額入りExcelを出力し、出力記録を保存しました。")}catch(error){alert(`Excelは出力しましたが、出力記録を保存できませんでした。\n${v161ErrorText(error)}`)}
};

function inventoryCountHome(msg=""){
  setScreen("inventoryCountHome",[msg]);if(!v119CanCountView())return home();v119EnsureAllCountSessions();const sessions=countSessions().slice().sort((a,b)=>new Date(b.updatedAt||0)-new Date(a.updatedAt||0)),active=sessions.filter(s=>["counting","submitted","qr_confirmed_pending_normal","normal_reversed_pending_qr"].includes(s.status)),history=sessions.filter(s=>!active.includes(s));
  const card=session=>{const stats=countStats(session),confirmed=session.status==="confirmed"&&!session.reversedAt,priceAllowed=confirmed&&v119CanPriceInput(),excel=confirmed&&session.priceStatus==="priced",doc=confirmed&&canAdmin()?`<button class="btn green" onclick="v119ConfirmInventoryCountDocument('${esc(session.id)}')">${session.documentSavedAt?"文書管理保存済み":"文書管理の保存確認"}</button>`:"",purge=v119CanDeleteCountDetails(session)?`<button class="btn red" onclick="v119DeleteInventoryCountDetails('${esc(session.id)}')">3か月経過後の明細削除</button>`:"";return`<div class="card"><div class="itemHead"><div><b>${esc(session.countNo)}</b><div class="small">${esc(session.roomLabel)} / ${esc(v119CountStatus(session))}<br>開始：${esc(session.startedAt?dt(session.startedAt):"-")} / 通常入力 ${stats.entered}/${stats.total}</div></div><span class="badge ${session.status==="confirmed"?"normal":session.status==="submitted"?"excess":"unset"}">${esc(v119CountStatus(session))}</span></div>${session.documentSavedAt?`<div class="small" style="margin-top:7px">文書管理保存済み：${esc(session.documentSavedPath||"")} / ${esc(dt(session.documentSavedAt))}</div>`:""}<div class="mini" style="margin-top:10px"><button class="btn ${session.status==="submitted"&&canAdmin()?"orange":"green"}" onclick="${session.status==="submitted"&&canAdmin()?`inventoryCountReviewPage('${esc(session.id)}')`:`inventoryCountPage('${esc(session.id)}')`}">${session.status==="submitted"&&canAdmin()?"管理者確認":"開く"}</button>${priceAllowed?`<button class="btn blue" onclick="v119InventoryCountPricePage('${esc(session.id)}')">${session.priceStatus==="priced"?"単価確認":"単価入力"}</button>`:""}${excel?`<button class="btn blue" onclick="downloadInventoryCountExcel('${esc(session.id)}')">金額入りExcel</button>`:""}${doc}${purge}</div></div>`};
  const start=canInventoryCountInput()?`<div class="card"><b>棚卸しを開始（管理者・青果社員）</b><div class="grid" style="margin-top:12px"><button class="btn orange" onclick="startInventoryCount('room2')">第2冷蔵庫を開始</button><button class="btn orange" onclick="startInventoryCount('room3')">第3冷蔵庫を開始</button></div></div>`:"";pageShell("棚卸しモード",`${esc(v119RoleLabel())} / ${V119_APP_VERSION}`,`${okHtml(msg)}<div class="card notice"><b>棚卸しの流れ</b><div class="small">①青果社員・管理者が実数確認 → ②管理者確定 → ③事務・営業・管理者が単価入力 → ④金額入りExcel → ⑤文書管理保存確認 → ⑥3か月経過後、管理者がサーバー側で明細削除</div></div>${start}<div class="card"><b>進行中・確認待ち</b>${active.map(card).join("")||'<div class="small">進行中の棚卸しはありません。</div>'}</div><div class="card"><b>履歴</b>${history.map(card).join("")||'<div class="small">履歴はまだありません。</div>'}</div>`);
}
function v119QrCountInput(id,key){
  const session=countSession(id),line=qrCountFindLine(session,key);if(!session||!line||!countEditable(session))return;
  const actualEl=document.getElementById("qrcount_actual_"+line.lineId),weightEl=document.getElementById("qrcount_weight_"+line.lineId),outEl=document.getElementById("qrcount_out_"+line.lineId),destEl=document.getElementById("qrcount_dest_"+line.lineId),timingEl=document.getElementById("qrcount_timing_"+line.lineId),memoEl=document.getElementById("qrcount_memo_"+line.lineId),raw=String(actualEl?.value??"").trim(),weightRaw=String(weightEl?.value??"").trim(),outRaw=String(outEl?.value??"").trim(),large=qrIsLargeContainer(line.containerType),weightMode=qrCountLargeWeightMode(line);
  if(!raw){line.actualQty=null;if(large)line.actualWeightKg=null;line.status="unentered"}else{
    const actual=v119StrictDecimal(raw),out=outRaw===""?0:v119StrictDecimal(outRaw),weight=large&&weightMode==="manual"?v119StrictDecimal(weightRaw):null;
    if(actual===null||actual<0)return v161FocusField("qrcount_actual_"+line.lineId,"実数は0以上の数値で入力してください。");
    if(out===null||out<0||out>Number(line.systemQty))return v161FocusField("qrcount_out_"+line.lineId,`簡単出庫は開始時帳簿 ${fmt(line.systemQty)} ${line.unit} 以下で入力してください。`);
    if(large&&weightMode==="manual"&&(weight===null||weight<0))return v161FocusField("qrcount_weight_"+line.lineId,"大型容器の棚卸し重量は0以上で入力してください（0も入力できます）。");
    if(out>0&&actual!==Number(line.systemQty)-out)return v161FocusField("qrcount_actual_"+line.lineId,`簡単出庫を使う場合、実数は開始時帳簿−出庫数（${fmt(Number(line.systemQty)-out)} ${line.unit}）にしてください。`);
    if(out>0&&(!destEl?.value||(destEl.value!=="廃棄"&&!timingEl?.value)))return alert("簡単出庫は出庫先と当日分／翌日分先送りを選択してください。");
    line.actualQty=yen(actual);if(large)line.actualWeightKg=weightMode==="auto"?qrCountActualWeight(line):yen(weight);line.outQty=yen(out);line.destination=destEl?.value||"";line.shipTiming=timingEl?.value||"";line.memo=memoEl?.value.trim()||"";line.status="entered";line.countedAt=nowIso();line.countedBy=db.user;line.countedById=db.userId;
  }
  v119SaveSessionLocal(session,"QR実数入力",`${line.itemName}: ${line.actualQty===null?"未入力":fmt(line.actualQty)+line.unit}`);inventoryCountPage(id);
}

function v119PalletDataFromEditor(lot){const inputs=[...document.querySelectorAll(".v119PalletQty")],qtys=inputs.map(input=>v119StrictDecimal(input.value));if(!inputs.length)return null;if(qtys.some(value=>value===null||value<=0))throw new Error("パレット別数量はすべて0より大きい数値で入力してください。");const received=v119StrictDecimal(lot.received_qty??lot.initial_quantity),sum=yen(qtys.reduce((total,value)=>total+value,0));if(received===null||received<=0||Math.abs(sum-received)>0.000001)throw new Error(`パレット別数量の合計 ${fmt(sum)} が入庫数量 ${fmt(received||0)} と一致しません。`);return{palletQtys:qtys.map(yen),totalQty:yen(received),unit:lot.unit||"ケース"}}

function v121Clone(v){return v===undefined?undefined:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(v))}
function v121Equal(a,b){return JSON.stringify(a)===JSON.stringify(b)}
function v121TestSession(s){return Boolean(s&&s.isTest===true&&s.mode==="test")}
function v121SessionKey(s){return String(s?.id||"")}
function v121LineKey(line,isQr){return String(isQr?(line?.qrKey||line?.qr_key||line?.id||""):(line?.lineId||line?.id||""))}
function v121Stamp(v){const n=Date.parse(v||"");return Number.isFinite(n)?n:-Infinity}
function v121LatestMeta(base,local,remote){const ls=v121Stamp(local?.updatedAt),rs=v121Stamp(remote?.updatedAt),pick=ls>=rs?local:remote;return{updatedAt:ls>=rs?(local?.updatedAt||remote?.updatedAt||base?.updatedAt):(remote?.updatedAt||local?.updatedAt||base?.updatedAt),updatedBy:pick?.updatedBy||base?.updatedBy||""}}
function v121ConflictLabel(session,row,isQr,field){const target=isQr?(row?.lotNo||row?.lot_no||row?.qrKey||row?.qr_key||row?.itemName||"不明ロット"):(row?.itemName||row?.name||row?.lineId||"不明品目");return`${isQr?"QRロット":"品目"}「${target}」の${field}`}
function v121PickValue(base,local,remote,label,conflicts){if(v121Equal(local,base))return v121Clone(remote);if(v121Equal(remote,base)||v121Equal(local,remote))return v121Clone(local);conflicts.push(label);return v121Clone(local)}
function v121MergeTestRow(base,local,remote,session,isQr,conflicts){
  if(local===undefined&&remote===undefined)return undefined;
  if(base===undefined){if(local===undefined)return v121Clone(remote);if(remote===undefined)return v121Clone(local);if(v121Equal(local,remote))return v121Clone(local);conflicts.push(v121ConflictLabel(session,local||remote,isQr,"新規行"));return v121Clone(local)}
  if(local===undefined||remote===undefined)return v121PickValue(base,local,remote,v121ConflictLabel(session,base,isQr,"行の削除"),conflicts);
  const out={},keys=new Set([...Object.keys(base||{}),...Object.keys(local||{}),...Object.keys(remote||{})]);
  const metadata=new Set(["revision","updatedAt","updatedBy","lastUpdatedAt","lastUpdatedBy","inputAt","inputBy"]);
  for(const key of keys){
    if(key==="revision"){out[key]=Math.max(Number(base?.[key])||0,Number(local?.[key])||0,Number(remote?.[key])||0)+1;continue}
    if(key==="updatedAt"||key==="lastUpdatedAt"||key==="inputAt"){const a=local?.[key],b=remote?.[key];out[key]=v121Stamp(a)>=v121Stamp(b)?(a||b||base?.[key]):(b||a||base?.[key]);continue}
    if(key==="updatedBy"||key==="lastUpdatedBy"||key==="inputBy"){const a=v121Stamp(local?.updatedAt),b=v121Stamp(remote?.updatedAt);out[key]=(a>=b?local?.[key]:remote?.[key])||base?.[key]||"";continue}
    const business=/^(actualQty|detailMode|packKg|caseCount|remainderKg|supplier|supplierRequired|kgPerUnit|unitPrice|unitPriceYenPerKg|count|amount|amountYen|countedKg|memo|palletQtys|packWeight)$/i.test(key);
    out[key]=business?v121PickValue(base?.[key],local?.[key],remote?.[key],v121ConflictLabel(session,local||remote||base,isQr,key),conflicts):v121Clone(remote?.[key]!==undefined?remote[key]:(local?.[key]!==undefined?local[key]:base?.[key]));
  }
  return out
}
function v121MergeTestRows(base,local,remote,session,isQr,conflicts){
  const keyOf=x=>v121LineKey(x,isQr),valid=a=>(a||[]).every(x=>keyOf(x));
  base=Array.isArray(base)?base:[];local=Array.isArray(local)?local:[];remote=Array.isArray(remote)?remote:[];
  if(!valid(base)||!valid(local)||!valid(remote)){conflicts.push(`${session?.countNo||"テスト棚卸し"}の${isQr?"QRロット":"品目"}行識別子`);return v121Clone(local)}
  const bm=new Map(base.map(x=>[keyOf(x),x])),lm=new Map(local.map(x=>[keyOf(x),x])),rm=new Map(remote.map(x=>[keyOf(x),x]));
  const order=[...remote,...local,...base].map(keyOf),seen=new Set(),out=[];
  for(const key of order){if(seen.has(key))continue;seen.add(key);const row=v121MergeTestRow(bm.get(key),lm.get(key),rm.get(key),session,isQr,conflicts);if(row!==undefined)out.push(row)}return out
}
function v121MergeTestSession(base,local,remote,conflicts){
  if(local===undefined&&remote===undefined)return undefined;
  if(!base){if(local===undefined)return v121Clone(remote);if(remote===undefined)return v121Clone(local);if(v121Equal(local,remote))return v121Clone(local);conflicts.push(`テスト棚卸し「${local?.countNo||remote?.countNo||"新規"}」を両端末で同時作成`);return v121Clone(local)}
  if(local===undefined||remote===undefined)return v121PickValue(base,local,remote,`テスト棚卸し「${base.countNo||base.id}」の削除`,conflicts);
  const out={},keys=new Set([...Object.keys(base),...Object.keys(local),...Object.keys(remote)]),meta=new Set(["revision","updatedAt","updatedBy","lastUpdatedAt","lastUpdatedBy"]);
  for(const key of keys){
    if(key==="lines"){out[key]=v121MergeTestRows(base[key],local[key],remote[key],local||remote,false,conflicts);continue}
    if(key==="qrLines"){out[key]=v121MergeTestRows(base[key],local[key],remote[key],local||remote,true,conflicts);continue}
    if(key==="revision"){out[key]=Math.max(Number(base[key])||0,Number(local[key])||0,Number(remote[key])||0)+1;continue}
    if(key==="updatedAt"||key==="lastUpdatedAt"){out[key]=v121Stamp(local[key])>=v121Stamp(remote[key])?(local[key]||remote[key]||base[key]):(remote[key]||local[key]||base[key]);continue}
    if(key==="updatedBy"||key==="lastUpdatedBy"){out[key]=(v121Stamp(local.updatedAt)>=v121Stamp(remote.updatedAt)?local[key]:remote[key])||base[key]||"";continue}
    out[key]=v121PickValue(base[key],local[key],remote[key],`テスト棚卸し「${local.countNo||remote.countNo||base.countNo||base.id}」の${key}`,conflicts);
  }return out
}
function v121MergeSessions(base,local,remote,conflicts){
  base=Array.isArray(base)?base:[];local=Array.isArray(local)?local:[];remote=Array.isArray(remote)?remote:[];
  const all=[...base,...local,...remote];if(all.some(x=>!v121SessionKey(x))){conflicts.push("棚卸しセッションID");return v121Clone(local)}
  const bm=new Map(base.map(x=>[v121SessionKey(x),x])),lm=new Map(local.map(x=>[v121SessionKey(x),x])),rm=new Map(remote.map(x=>[v121SessionKey(x),x]));
  const order=[...remote,...local,...base].map(v121SessionKey),seen=new Set(),out=[];
  for(const id of order){if(seen.has(id))continue;seen.add(id);const b=bm.get(id),l=lm.get(id),r=rm.get(id),test=[b,l,r].some(v121TestSession);let row;
    if(test)row=v121MergeTestSession(b,l,r,conflicts);else{const normal=v188MergePayload({session:b},{session:l},{session:r});if(!normal.ok)conflicts.push(...normal.conflicts.map(x=>`本番棚卸し「${l?.countNo||r?.countNo||b?.countNo||id}」`));row=normal.merged.session}
    if(row!==undefined)out.push(row)}return out
}
function v121MergeInventoryCounts(base,local,remote){
  base=base&&typeof base==="object"?base:{};local=local&&typeof local==="object"?local:{};remote=remote&&typeof remote==="object"?remote:{};
  const conflicts=[],out={},keys=new Set([...Object.keys(base),...Object.keys(local),...Object.keys(remote)]);
  for(const key of keys){if(key==="sessions"){out.sessions=v121MergeSessions(base.sessions,local.sessions,remote.sessions,conflicts);continue}
    const result=v188MergePayload({value:base[key]},{value:local[key]},{value:remote[key]});if(!result.ok)conflicts.push(...result.conflicts.map(x=>`棚卸し設定「${key}」`));out[key]=result.merged.value}
  return{ok:conflicts.length===0,merged:out,conflicts:[...new Set(conflicts)].slice(0,12)}
}
function v121MergePayload(base,local,remote){
  const b=v121Clone(base||{}),l=v121Clone(local||{}),r=v121Clone(remote||{}),hasInventory=[b,l,r].some(x=>Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(x,"inventoryCounts"));
  if(!hasInventory)return v188MergePayload(b,l,r);
  delete b.inventoryCounts;delete l.inventoryCounts;delete r.inventoryCounts;
  const standard=v188MergePayload(b,l,r),inventory=v121MergeInventoryCounts(base?.inventoryCounts,local?.inventoryCounts,remote?.inventoryCounts);
  return{ok:standard.ok&&inventory.ok,merged:{...v121Clone(remote||{}),...standard.merged,inventoryCounts:inventory.merged},conflicts:[...new Set([...(standard.conflicts||[]),...(inventory.conflicts||[])])].slice(0,12)}
}
function v121RestoreBase(item){const current=item?.basePayload;if(current)return v121Clone(current);const bundleBase=v188SyncBundle?.base?.payload;if(bundleBase){item.basePayload=v121Clone(bundleBase);item.legacyBaseUnavailable=false;return v121Clone(bundleBase)}return null}
function v121ConflictMessage(conflicts){return `同じ入力が別端末でも変更されています：${(conflicts||[]).join("、")}`}
v178ResolvePendingInventoryConflict=async function(){
  await v188IdbFlush();v188AssertPendingOwner();const original=v161ReadQueue()[0];if(!original)return{ok:true,merged:0};
  const base=v121RestoreBase(original);if(!base){setSyncState("競合","この端末には統合基準がありません。未同期入力は保持しています");throw new Error("この端末には統合基準がありません。未同期入力は削除せず保持しています。管理者へ連絡してください。")}
  const remote=await v188FetchRemoteState(),local=v121Clone(original.localPayload||original.payload),baseAllowed=v188RolePayload(base),localAllowed=v188RolePayload(local),remoteAllowed=v188RolePayload(remote.payload),result=v121MergePayload(baseAllowed,localAllowed,remoteAllowed);
  if(!result.ok){setSyncState("競合","同じ品目・ロットの入力を確認してください。端末データは保持しています");throw new Error(v121ConflictMessage(result.conflicts))}
  const fullMerged={...v121Clone(remote.payload),...v121Clone(result.merged)},candidate={...original,id:newId(),operationId:newId(),expectedVersion:remote.version,baseVersion:remote.version,basePayload:v121Clone(remote.payload),payload:v121Clone(fullMerged),localPayload:v121Clone(fullMerged),mergeCandidate:v121Clone(fullMerged),lastError:"",updatedAt:nowIso()};
  try{const ans=await v188SendSyncItem(candidate,candidate.localPayload,remote.version);if(ans?.conflict)throw new Error("統合中に別の更新が入りました。元の入力を保持したまま、もう一度再送してください。");if(!ans?.ok)throw new Error(ans?.message||"統合データを保存できませんでした");stateVersion=Number(ans.version);const auth={user:db.user,userId:db.userId,role:db.role};db=normalize({...candidate.localPayload,...auth});v188PersistDb();v188WriteBase(candidate.localPayload,stateVersion);v161WriteQueue([]);markSync();setSyncState("保存済み","テスト棚卸し入力を他端末の更新と統合しました");if(historyTablesReady){try{await saveHistoryTablesNow();v188SetHistoryPending(false)}catch(e){v188SetHistoryPending(true);setSyncState("未同期","統合済み履歴を再送待ち")}}await v188IdbFlush();return{ok:true,merged:1}
  }catch(e){original.lastError=v161ErrorText(e);v161WriteQueue([original]);await v188IdbFlush();throw e}
};

const V122_APP_VERSION="v1.122"; APP_VERSION=V122_APP_VERSION; window.APP_VERSION=V122_APP_VERSION; document.title="SEIKA＋ v1.122 棚卸し同期救出修正版";
function v122Own(o,k){return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(o||{},k)}
function v122Blank(v){return v===undefined||v===null||(typeof v==="string"&&v.trim()==="")}
function v122Number(v){if(v122Blank(v))return 0;const n=Number(v);return Number.isFinite(n)?n:v}
function v122Actual(v){if(v122Blank(v))return {unset:true};const n=Number(v);return Number.isFinite(n)?n:v}
function v122Text(v){return v122Blank(v)?"":String(v).trim()}
function v122Boolean(v){if(v===true||v===1||v==="1"||String(v).toLowerCase()==="true")return true;return false}
function v122Comparable(value,key,isQr){
  if(key==="actualQty")return v122Actual(value);
  if(key==="detailMode")return v122Blank(value)?"total":String(value).trim();
  if(["packKg","caseCount","remainderKg","kgPerUnit","unitPrice","unitPriceYenPerKg","countedKg","amount","amountYen","count","packWeight"].includes(key))return v122Number(value);
  if(key==="supplier")return v122Text(value);
  if(key==="supplierRequired")return v122Boolean(value);
  if(key==="memo")return v122Text(value);
  if(key==="palletQtys")return Array.isArray(value)?value.map(v122Number):[];
  return value;
}
function v122EqualValue(a,b){return JSON.stringify(a)===JSON.stringify(b)}
function v122PresentRaw(v,key){if(key==="actualQty")return !v122Blank(v);if(Array.isArray(v))return v.length>0;return !v122Blank(v)}
function v122PreferredRaw(local,remote,key){
  const lp=v122PresentRaw(local,key),rp=v122PresentRaw(remote,key);
  if(lp&&!rp)return v121Clone(local);if(rp&&!lp)return v121Clone(remote);
  return v121Clone(local!==undefined?local:remote);
}
function v122Label(session,row,isQr,key){return v121ConflictLabel(session,row,isQr,key==="actualQty"?"実数":key)}
const V122_INPUT_FIELDS=new Set(["actualQty","detailMode","packKg","caseCount","remainderKg","supplier","supplierRequired","kgPerUnit","unitPrice","unitPriceYenPerKg","countedKg","amount","amountYen","count","memo","palletQtys","packWeight"]);
const V122_METADATA_FIELDS=new Set(["revision","updatedAt","updatedBy","lastUpdatedAt","lastUpdatedBy","inputAt","inputBy","snapshotCases","systemQty","difference","differenceQty","snapshotQty","currentQty","expectedQty","snapshotKg","systemKg","displayQty"]);
function v122MergeInput(base,local,remote,key,session,row,isQr,conflicts){
  const b=v122Comparable(base,key,isQr),l=v122Comparable(local,key,isQr),r=v122Comparable(remote,key,isQr);
  if(v122EqualValue(l,b)&&v122EqualValue(r,b))return v122PreferredRaw(local,remote,key);
  if(v122EqualValue(l,b))return v121Clone(remote);
  if(v122EqualValue(r,b))return v121Clone(local);
  if(v122EqualValue(l,r))return v122PreferredRaw(local,remote,key);
  conflicts.push(v122Label(session,row,isQr,key));return v121Clone(local);
}
function v122Derived(base,local,remote){return v121Clone(remote!==undefined?remote:(local!==undefined?local:base))}
function v122MergeTestRow(base,local,remote,session,isQr,conflicts){
  if(local===undefined&&remote===undefined)return undefined;
  if(base===undefined){
    if(local===undefined)return v121Clone(remote);if(remote===undefined)return v121Clone(local);
    /* same newly created row: semantic comparison prevents blank/default false positives */
    const keys=new Set([...Object.keys(local||{}),...Object.keys(remote||{})]);let same=true;
    for(const key of keys){if(V122_INPUT_FIELDS.has(key)&&!v122EqualValue(v122Comparable(local[key],key,isQr),v122Comparable(remote[key],key,isQr))){same=false;break}}
    if(same)return v121Clone(v122PresentRaw(local?.actualQty,"actualQty")?local:remote);
    conflicts.push(v121ConflictLabel(session,local||remote,isQr,"新規行"));return v121Clone(local);
  }
  if(local===undefined||remote===undefined)return v121PickValue(base,local,remote,v121ConflictLabel(session,base,isQr,"行の削除"),conflicts);
  const out={},keys=new Set([...Object.keys(base||{}),...Object.keys(local||{}),...Object.keys(remote||{})]);
  for(const key of keys){
    if(key==="revision"){out[key]=Math.max(Number(base?.[key])||0,Number(local?.[key])||0,Number(remote?.[key])||0)+1;continue}
    if(key==="updatedAt"||key==="lastUpdatedAt"||key==="inputAt"){const a=local?.[key],b=remote?.[key];out[key]=v121Stamp(a)>=v121Stamp(b)?(a||b||base?.[key]):(b||a||base?.[key]);continue}
    if(key==="updatedBy"||key==="lastUpdatedBy"||key==="inputBy"){out[key]=(v121Stamp(local?.updatedAt)>=v121Stamp(remote?.updatedAt)?local?.[key]:remote?.[key])||base?.[key]||"";continue}
    if(V122_INPUT_FIELDS.has(key)){out[key]=v122MergeInput(base?.[key],local?.[key],remote?.[key],key,session,local||remote||base,isQr,conflicts);continue}
    if(V122_METADATA_FIELDS.has(key)){out[key]=v122Derived(base?.[key],local?.[key],remote?.[key]);continue}
    out[key]=v122Derived(base?.[key],local?.[key],remote?.[key]);
  }
  return out;
}
function v122MergeStatus(base,local,remote,session,key,conflicts){
  if(v121Equal(local,base))return v121Clone(remote);
  if(v121Equal(remote,base)||v121Equal(local,remote))return v121Clone(local);
  conflicts.push(`テスト棚卸し「${local?.countNo||remote?.countNo||base?.countNo||base?.id}」の${key}`);return v121Clone(local);
}
function v122MergeTestSession(base,local,remote,conflicts){
  if(local===undefined&&remote===undefined)return undefined;
  if(!base){if(local===undefined)return v121Clone(remote);if(remote===undefined)return v121Clone(local);if(v121Equal(local,remote))return v121Clone(local);conflicts.push(`テスト棚卸し「${local?.countNo||remote?.countNo||"新規"}」を両端末で同時作成`);return v121Clone(local)}
  if(local===undefined||remote===undefined)return v121PickValue(base,local,remote,`テスト棚卸し「${base.countNo||base.id}」の削除`,conflicts);
  const out={},keys=new Set([...Object.keys(base),...Object.keys(local),...Object.keys(remote)]);
  for(const key of keys){
    if(key==="lines"){out[key]=v121MergeTestRows(base[key],local[key],remote[key],local||remote,false,conflicts);continue}
    if(key==="qrLines"){out[key]=v121MergeTestRows(base[key],local[key],remote[key],local||remote,true,conflicts);continue}
    if(key==="revision"){out[key]=Math.max(Number(base[key])||0,Number(local[key])||0,Number(remote[key])||0)+1;continue}
    if(key==="updatedAt"||key==="lastUpdatedAt"||key==="submittedAt"||key==="confirmedAt"){out[key]=v122Derived(base[key],local[key],remote[key]);continue}
    if(key==="updatedBy"||key==="lastUpdatedBy"||key==="submittedBy"||key==="confirmedBy"){out[key]=(v121Stamp(local.updatedAt)>=v121Stamp(remote.updatedAt)?local[key]:remote[key])||base[key]||"";continue}
    if(["status","qrStatus","priceStatus"].includes(key)){out[key]=v122MergeStatus(base[key],local[key],remote[key],base,key,conflicts);continue}
    out[key]=v122Derived(base[key],local[key],remote[key]);
  }
  return out;
}
/* Existing session/queue functions call these names at runtime; overriding them keeps v1.121 queues readable. */
v121MergeTestRow=v122MergeTestRow;
v121MergeTestSession=v122MergeTestSession;

/* ===== v1.124 棚卸し片岡分離・統合Excel ===== */
const V123_APP_VERSION="v1.123";
window.APP_VERSION=V123_APP_VERSION;
document.title="SEIKA＋ v1.123 棚卸し分担・一括保存";
const V123_DRAFT_PREFIX="seika_inventory_count_draft_v123";
const v123Clone=x=>x===undefined?undefined:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(x));
const v123Now=()=>nowIso();
function v123Test(s){return Boolean(s&&s.isTest===true&&s.mode==="test")}
function v123SectionStatus(s,section){
  v123NormalizeSession(s);
  return section==="normal"?s.normalStatus:s.qrCountStatus;
}
function v123Editable(s,section){
  const status=v123SectionStatus(s,section);
  return canInventoryCountInput()&&["counting","incomplete"].includes(status);
}
function v123SectionLabel(section){return section==="normal"?"通常・その他在庫":"QR在庫"}
function v123CompletedStatus(s){return v123Test(s)?"test_submitted":"submitted"}
function v123CountingStatus(s){return v123Test(s)?"test_counting":"counting"}
function v123NormalizeWeightEntries(line){
  if(!v119IsKgLine(line))return line;
  if(!Array.isArray(line.weightEntries)){
    const pack=v119StrictDecimal(line.packKg),cases=v119StrictDecimal(line.caseCount),actual=v119StrictDecimal(line.actualQty);
    line.weightEntries=(pack!==null&&pack>0&&cases!==null&&cases>=0)?[{id:"legacy-1",packKg:pack,caseCount:cases}]:(actual!==null?[{id:"legacy-1",packKg:actual>0?actual:1,caseCount:actual>0?1:0}]:[{id:"w-1",packKg:"",caseCount:""}]);
  }
  line.weightEntries=line.weightEntries.map((e,index)=>({id:String(e?.id||`w-${index+1}`),packKg:e?.packKg??"",caseCount:e?.caseCount??""}));
  if(!line.weightEntries.length)line.weightEntries=[{id:"w-1",packKg:"",caseCount:""}];
  if(line.remainderKg===undefined||line.remainderKg===null)line.remainderKg="";
  line.inputMode="weight_entries";
  return line;
}
function v123NormalizeSession(s){
  if(!s||typeof s!=="object")return s;
  const test=v123Test(s),old=s.status||"";
  const legacyCounting=["counting","test_counting",""].includes(old);
  const legacySubmitted=["submitted","test_submitted"].includes(old);
  const legacyConfirmed=["confirmed","test_confirmed"].includes(old);
  s.normalStatus=s.normalStatus|| (legacyConfirmed?"confirmed":legacySubmitted?"submitted":legacyCounting?"counting":"counting");
  s.qrCountStatus=s.qrCountStatus|| (legacyConfirmed?"confirmed":legacySubmitted?"submitted":legacyCounting?"counting":"counting");
  s.normalRevision=Math.max(0,Number(s.normalRevision||s.revision||0));
  s.qrRevision=Math.max(0,Number(s.qrRevision||s.revision||0));
  (s.lines||[]).forEach(l=>{v119EnsureCountLine(l);v123NormalizeWeightEntries(l)});
  (s.qrLines||[]).forEach(v119EnsureCountLine);
  v123DeriveStatus(s);return s;
}
function v123DeriveStatus(s){
  if(!s)return s;const test=v123Test(s),submitted=v123CompletedStatus(s),confirmed=test?"test_confirmed":"confirmed";
  if(s.normalStatus==="confirmed"&&s.qrCountStatus==="confirmed")s.status=confirmed;
  else if(s.normalStatus==="submitted"&&s.qrCountStatus==="submitted")s.status=submitted;
  else if(s.status!=="cancelled")s.status=v123CountingStatus(s);
  return s;
}
function v123AllSessions(){countSessions().forEach(v123NormalizeSession)}
function v123DraftKey(id,section){return `${V123_DRAFT_PREFIX}:${db?.userId||"anonymous"}:${id}:${section}`}
function v123ReadDraft(id,section){try{return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(v123DraftKey(id,section))||"null")}catch{return null}}
function v123WriteDraft(id,section,entries){try{localStorage.setItem(v123DraftKey(id,section),JSON.stringify({at:v123Now(),entries:v123Clone(entries||[])}))}catch(error){console.warn("棚卸し下書きの端末保存に失敗",error)}}
function v123ClearDraft(id,section){try{localStorage.removeItem(v123DraftKey(id,section))}catch{}}
function v123LineId(line,section){return String(section==="qr"?(line?.qrKey||line?.qr_key||line?.lineId||""):(line?.lineId||line?.id||""))}
function v123Entered(line){return line?.actualQty!==null&&line?.actualQty!==undefined&&String(line.actualQty)!==""}
function v123SectionStats(s,section){const rows=section==="normal"?(s.lines||[]):(s.qrLines||[]),entered=rows.filter(v123Entered).length;return{total:rows.length,entered,missing:rows.length-entered,diff:rows.filter(l=>v123Entered(l)&&Number(l.actualQty)!==Number(l.systemQty||0)).length}}
function v123EntriesFromSession(s,section){return (section==="normal"?s.lines:s.qrLines||[]).map(line=>{
 const id=v123LineId(line,section);const base={id,actualQty:line.actualQty??null,memo:line.memo||"",supplier:line.supplier||"",supplierRequired:!!line.supplierRequired};
 if(section==="normal")return {...base,weightEntries:v123Clone(line.weightEntries||[]),remainderKg:line.remainderKg??"",kgPerUnit:line.kgPerUnit??""};
 return {...base,actualWeightKg:line.actualWeightKg??null,outQty:line.outQty??0,destination:line.destination||"",shipTiming:line.shipTiming||""};
 })}
function v123DraftEntries(s,section){const draft=v123ReadDraft(s.id,section);return Array.isArray(draft?.entries)?draft.entries:v123EntriesFromSession(s,section)}
function v123EntryMap(s,section){return new Map(v123DraftEntries(s,section).map(e=>[String(e.id),e]))}
function v123WeightTotal(entries,remainder){let total=0,has=false;for(const e of entries||[]){const pack=v119StrictDecimal(e.packKg),cases=v119StrictDecimal(e.caseCount);if(String(e.packKg??"").trim()||String(e.caseCount??"").trim()){if(pack===null||pack<=0||cases===null||cases<0)return null;total+=pack*cases;has=true}}const rem=String(remainder??"").trim();if(rem!==""){const n=v119StrictDecimal(rem);if(n===null||n<0)return null;total+=n;has=true}return has?yen(total):null}
function v123ApplyEntries(s,section,entries){
 const lines=section==="normal"?s.lines:s.qrLines,seen=new Set();
 for(const entry of entries||[]){const id=String(entry?.id||"");if(!id||seen.has(id))continue;seen.add(id);const line=(lines||[]).find(x=>v123LineId(x,section)===id);if(!line)continue;
 if(section==="normal"){
   if(v119IsKgLine(line)){line.weightEntries=v123Clone(entry.weightEntries||[{id:"w-1",packKg:"",caseCount:""}]);line.remainderKg=entry.remainderKg??"";line.actualQty=v123WeightTotal(line.weightEntries,line.remainderKg)}
   else {line.actualQty=entry.actualQty===null||entry.actualQty===undefined||String(entry.actualQty)===""?null:v119StrictDecimal(entry.actualQty);line.kgPerUnit=entry.kgPerUnit??""}
   line.supplier=String(entry.supplier||"").trim();line.supplierRequired=!!entry.supplierRequired;line.memo=String(entry.memo||"").trim();
 }else {line.actualQty=entry.actualQty===null||entry.actualQty===undefined||String(entry.actualQty)===""?null:v119StrictDecimal(entry.actualQty);line.actualWeightKg=entry.actualWeightKg===null||entry.actualWeightKg===undefined||String(entry.actualWeightKg)===""?null:v119StrictDecimal(entry.actualWeightKg);line.outQty=entry.outQty??0;line.destination=entry.destination||"";line.shipTiming=entry.shipTiming||"";line.memo=entry.memo||""}
 line.status=v123Entered(line)?"entered":"unentered";
 }
 return s;
}
function v123PersistDom(id,section){const s=countSession(id);if(!s)return;const old=v123EntryMap(s,section),lines=section==="normal"?s.lines:s.qrLines;
 (lines||[]).forEach(line=>{const key=v123LineId(line,section),entry={...(old.get(key)||{id:key})};if(section==="normal"){
   if(v119IsKgLine(line)){entry.weightEntries=[...document.querySelectorAll(`[data-v123-weight="${CSS.escape(key)}"]`)].map(x=>({id:x.dataset.entryId,packKg:document.getElementById(`v123_pack_${key}_${x.dataset.entryId}`)?.value??"",caseCount:document.getElementById(`v123_case_${key}_${x.dataset.entryId}`)?.value??""}));entry.remainderKg=document.getElementById(`v123_rem_${key}`)?.value??"";entry.actualQty=v123WeightTotal(entry.weightEntries,entry.remainderKg)}else{const x=document.getElementById(`v123_qty_${key}`);entry.actualQty=x?x.value:"";entry.kgPerUnit=document.getElementById(`v123_kgper_${key}`)?.value??""}entry.supplier=document.getElementById(`v123_supplier_${key}`)?.value??"";entry.supplierRequired=!!document.getElementById(`v123_required_${key}`)?.checked;entry.memo=document.getElementById(`v123_memo_${key}`)?.value??"";
 }else{entry.actualQty=document.getElementById(`v123_qty_${key}`)?.value??"";entry.actualWeightKg=document.getElementById(`v123_weight_${key}`)?.value??"";entry.outQty=document.getElementById(`v123_out_${key}`)?.value??"";entry.destination=document.getElementById(`v123_dest_${key}`)?.value??"";entry.shipTiming=document.getElementById(`v123_timing_${key}`)?.value??"";entry.memo=document.getElementById(`v123_memo_${key}`)?.value??"";}old.set(key,entry)});v123WriteDraft(id,section,[...old.values()]);
}
function v123Missing(s,section){const entries=v123DraftEntries(s,section),base=section==="normal"?s.lines||[]:s.qrLines||[];return base.filter(line=>{const e=entries.find(x=>String(x.id)===v123LineId(line,section));if(!e)return true;const actual=section==="normal"&&v119IsKgLine(line)?v123WeightTotal(e.weightEntries,e.remainderKg):v119StrictDecimal(e.actualQty);if(actual===null)return true;if(section==="normal"&&e.supplierRequired&&!String(e.supplier||"").trim())return true;if(section==="qr"&&qrIsLargeContainer(line.containerType)&&qrCountLargeWeightMode(line)==="manual"&&String(e.actualWeightKg??"").trim()==="")return true;return false})}
function v123UpdateWeightTotal(id,lineId){v123PersistDom(id,"normal");const s=countSession(id),e=v123EntryMap(s,"normal").get(lineId),box=document.getElementById(`v123_total_${lineId}`),n=v123WeightTotal(e?.weightEntries,e?.remainderKg);if(box)box.textContent=n===null?"未入力":`${fmt(n)} kg`;}
function v123AddWeightEntry(id,lineId){v123PersistDom(id,"normal");const s=countSession(id),entries=v123EntryMap(s,"normal"),e=entries.get(lineId);e.weightEntries=e.weightEntries||[];e.weightEntries.push({id:`w-${Date.now()}-${e.weightEntries.length}`,packKg:"",caseCount:""});entries.set(lineId,e);v123WriteDraft(id,"normal",[...entries.values()]);v123NormalPage(id)}
function v123RemoveWeightEntry(id,lineId,entryId){v123PersistDom(id,"normal");const s=countSession(id),entries=v123EntryMap(s,"normal"),e=entries.get(lineId);e.weightEntries=(e.weightEntries||[]).filter(x=>x.id!==entryId);if(!e.weightEntries.length)e.weightEntries=[{id:"w-1",packKg:"",caseCount:""}];entries.set(lineId,e);v123WriteDraft(id,"normal",[...entries.values()]);v123NormalPage(id)}
function v123NormalLineHtml(s,line,editable,entry){const id=v123LineId(line,"normal"),kg=v119IsKgLine(line),actual=kg?v123WeightTotal(entry.weightEntries,entry.remainderKg):(String(entry.actualQty??"").trim()===""?null:v119StrictDecimal(entry.actualQty));const missing=actual===null;const diff=!missing?yen(actual-Number(line.systemQty||0)):null;const eRows=(entry.weightEntries||[{id:"w-1",packKg:"",caseCount:""}]).map((e,n)=>`<div class="row" data-v123-weight="${esc(id)}" data-entry-id="${esc(e.id)}" style="margin-top:6px"><div><label class="label">量目kg ${n+1}</label><input id="v123_pack_${esc(id)}_${esc(e.id)}" class="input" inputmode="decimal" value="${esc(e.packKg??"")}" oninput="v123UpdateWeightTotal('${esc(s.id)}','${esc(id)}')" ${editable?"":"disabled"}></div><div><label class="label">ケース数</label><input id="v123_case_${esc(id)}_${esc(e.id)}" class="input" inputmode="decimal" value="${esc(e.caseCount??"")}" oninput="v123UpdateWeightTotal('${esc(s.id)}','${esc(id)}')" ${editable?"":"disabled"}></div>${editable?`<div style="display:flex;align-items:end"><button type="button" class="btn btn2" onclick="v123RemoveWeightEntry('${esc(s.id)}','${esc(id)}','${esc(e.id)}')">削除</button></div>`:""}</div>`).join("");
 return `<section id="v123_line_${esc(id)}" class="card countLine ${missing?"v123Missing":""}"><div class="countMeta"><div><b>${esc(line.itemName)}</b><div class="small">${esc(line.group||"")} / 開始時帳簿：${fmt(line.systemQty)} ${esc(kg?"kg":line.unit||"単位")}</div></div><span class="badge ${missing?"unset":diff===0?"normal":diff<0?"shortage":"excess"}">${missing?"未入力":`${diff>0?"+":""}${fmt(diff)} ${esc(kg?"kg":line.unit||"")}`}</span></div>${kg?`<div class="small" style="margin-top:7px">量目kg × ケースを複数登録できます。空欄は未入力、0ケース・端数0kgは入力値です。</div>${eRows}${editable?`<button class="btn btn2" style="margin-top:7px" onclick="v123AddWeightEntry('${esc(s.id)}','${esc(id)}')">＋ 量目を追加</button>`:""}<div class="row" style="margin-top:7px"><div><label class="label">端数kg</label><input id="v123_rem_${esc(id)}" class="input" inputmode="decimal" value="${esc(entry.remainderKg??"")}" oninput="v123UpdateWeightTotal('${esc(s.id)}','${esc(id)}')" ${editable?"":"disabled"}></div><div><label class="label">合計実数</label><div class="input" id="v123_total_${esc(id)}" style="background:#f8fafc">${missing?"未入力":fmt(actual)+" kg"}</div></div></div>`:`<div class="row" style="margin-top:8px"><div><label class="label">実数（${esc(line.unit||"単位")}）</label><input id="v123_qty_${esc(id)}" class="input" inputmode="decimal" value="${esc(entry.actualQty??"")}" placeholder="空欄=未入力 / 0=実数ゼロ" oninput="v123PersistDom('${esc(s.id)}','normal')" ${editable?"":"disabled"}></div><div><label class="label">1${esc(line.unit||"単位")}あたりkg</label><input id="v123_kgper_${esc(id)}" class="input" inputmode="decimal" value="${esc(entry.kgPerUnit??"")}" placeholder="必要時" oninput="v123PersistDom('${esc(s.id)}','normal')" ${editable?"":"disabled"}></div></div>`}<div class="row" style="margin-top:7px"><div><label class="label">仕入先（必要時）</label><input id="v123_supplier_${esc(id)}" class="input" value="${esc(entry.supplier||"")}" oninput="v123PersistDom('${esc(s.id)}','normal')" ${editable?"":"disabled"}></div><div><label class="label">仕入先を必須にする</label><label class="checkRow"><input id="v123_required_${esc(id)}" type="checkbox" ${entry.supplierRequired?"checked":""} onchange="v123PersistDom('${esc(s.id)}','normal')" ${editable?"":"disabled"}> 要確認</label></div></div><label class="label" style="margin-top:7px">メモ</label><input id="v123_memo_${esc(id)}" class="input" value="${esc(entry.memo||"")}" oninput="v123PersistDom('${esc(s.id)}','normal')" ${editable?"":"disabled"}></section>`}
function v123QrLineHtml(s,line,editable,entry){const id=v123LineId(line,"qr"),large=qrIsLargeContainer(line.containerType),manualWeight=large&&qrCountLargeWeightMode(line)==="manual",hasQty=String(entry.actualQty??"").trim()!=="",missing=!hasQty||(manualWeight&&String(entry.actualWeightKg??"").trim()===""),diff=hasQty?Number(entry.actualQty)-Number(line.systemQty||0):null;return `<section id="v123_line_${esc(id)}" class="card countLine ${missing?"v123Missing":""}"><div class="countMeta"><div><b>${esc(line.itemName||"QRロット")}</b><div ${missing?"unset":diff===0?"normal":diff<0?"shortage":"excess"}">${missing?"未確認":`${diff>0?"+":""}${fmt(diff)} ${esc(line.unit||"")}`}</span></div><div class="row" style="margin-top:8px"><div><label class="label">実数（${esc(line.unit||"ケース")}）</label><input id="v123_qty_${esc(id)}" class="input" inputmode="decimal" value="${esc(entry.actualQty??"")}" placeholder="空欄=未確認 / 0=実数ゼロ" oninput="v123PersistDom('${esc(s.id)}','qr')" ${editable?"":"disabled"}></div>${large?`<div><label class="label">棚卸し重量kg${manualWeight?"（必須 / 0可）":"（自動計算）"}</label><input id="v123_weight_${esc(id)}" class="input" inputmode="decimal" value="${esc(entry.actualWeightKg??"")}" placeholder="${manualWeight?"必須 / 0可":"自動計算"}" oninput="v123PersistDom('${esc(s.id)}','qr')" ${editable?"":"disabled"} ${manualWeight?"":"readonly"}></div>`:""}</div><div class="row" style="margin-top:7px"><div><label class="label">簡単出庫（任意）</label><input id="v123_out_${esc(id)}" class="input" inputmode="decimal" value="${esc(entry.outQty??"")}" oninput="v123PersistDom('${esc(s.id)}','qr')" ${editable?"":"disabled"}></div><div><label class="label">出庫先</label><input id="v123_dest_${esc(id)}" class="input" const v124IsKataoka=l=>String(l?.tableId||'')==='kataoka'&&String(l?.type||'')==='special';
const v124SectionKey=section=>section==='normal'?'lines':section==='qr'?'qrLines':'kataokaLines';

const v124RevisionKey=section=>section==='normal'?'normalRevision':section==='qr'?'qrRevision':'kataokaRevision';

function v124SectionRows(s,section){return s?.[v124SectionKey(section)]||[]}
function v124NormalizeSession(s){
 if(!s||typeof s!=='object')return s; v123NormalizeSession(s);
 const isRoom2=s.room==='room2', legacyKataoka=(s.lines||[]).filter(v124IsKataoka), remaining=(s.lines||[]).filter(x=>!v124IsKataoka(x));

 } else { s.kataokaLines=[]; delete s.kataokaStatus; delete s.kataokaRevision; }
 s.countSchemaVersion=124; return v124DeriveStatus(s);
}
function v124DeriveStatus(s){if(!s)return s;const test=v123Test(s),all=v124Sections(s).every(x=>s[v124StatusKey(x)]==='confirmed');const submitted=v124Sections(s).every(x=>['submitted','confirmed'].includes(s[v124StatusKey(x)]));
 s.status=all?(test?'test_confirmed':'confirmed'):submitted?(test?'test_submitted':'submitted'):(test?'test_counting':'counting');return s}

function v124DraftKey(id,section){return `${V124_DRAFT_PREFIX}:${db?.userId||'anonymous'}:${id}:${section}`}
function v124ReadDraft(id,section){try{return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(v124DraftKey(id,section))||'null')}catch{return null}}
function v124WriteDraft(id,section,entries){try{localStorage.setItem(v124DraftKey(id,section),JSON.stringify({at:nowIso(),entries:v124Clone(entries||[])}))}catch(e){console.warn('端末下書き保存に失敗',e)}}

function v124WeightTotal(entries,remainder){let total=0,has=false;for(const e of entries||[]){const p=v119StrictDecimal(e?.packKg),c=v119StrictDecimal(e?.caseCount);if(String(e?.packKg??'').trim()||String(e?.caseCount??'').trim()){if(p===null||p<=0||c===null||c<0)return null;total+=p*c;has=true}}const r=String(remainder??'').trim();if(r!==''){const n=v119StrictDecimal(r);if(n===null||n<0)return null;total+=n;has=true}return has?Math.round(total*1000)/1000:null}
function v124EntriesFromSession(s,section){return v124SectionRows(s,section).map(line=>{const id=v124LineId(line,section),base={id,actualQty:line.actualQty??null,memo:line.memo||'',supplier:line.supplier||'',supplierRequired:!!line.supplierRequired};return section==='qr'?{...base,actualWeightKg:line.actualWeightKg??null,outQty:line.outQty??0,destination:line.destination||'',shipTiming:line.shipTiming||''}:{...base,weightEntries:v124Clone(line.weightEntries||[]),remainderKg:line.remainderKg??'',kgPerUnit:line.kgPerUnit??''}})}
