id=v124LineId(line,'qr'),large=qrIsLargeContainer(line.containerType),manual=large&&qrCountLargeWeightMode(line)==='manual',actual=v119StrictDecimal(entry.actualQty),missing=actual===null||(manual&&String(entry.actualWeightKg??'').trim()===''),diff=actual===null?null:actual-Number(line.systemQty||0);return `<section class="card countLine ${missing?'v123Missing':''}"><div class="countMeta"><div><b>${esc(line.itemName||'QRロット')}</b><div class="small">${esc(line.lotNo||line.qrKey||id)} / ${esc(line.containerType||'')} / 開始時：${fmt(line.systemQty)} ${esc(line.unit||'')}</div></div><span class="badge ${missing?'unset':diff===0?'normal':diff<0?'shortage':'excess'}">${missing?'未確認':`${diff>0?'+':''}${fmt(diff)} ${esc(line.unit||'')}`}</span></div><div class="row" style="margin-top:7px"><div><label class="label">実数（${esc(line.unit||'ケース')}）</label><input id="v124_qty_${esc(id)}" class="input" inputmode="decimal" value="${esc(entry.actualQty??'')}" oninput="v124PersistDom('${esc(s.id)}','qr')" ${editable?'':'disabled'}></div>${large?`<div><label class="label">棚卸し重量kg${manual?'（必須 / 0可）':'（自動計算）'}</label><input id="v124_weight_${esc(id)}" class="input" inputmode="decimal" value="${esc(entry.actualWeightKg??'')}" oninput="v124PersistDom('${esc(s.id)}','qr')" ${editable?'':'disabled'} ${manual?'':'readonly'}></div>`:''}</div><div class="row" style="margin-top:7px"><div><label class="label">簡単出庫</label><input id="v124_out_${esc(id)}" class="input" inputmode="decimal" value="${esc(entry.outQty??'')}" oninput="v124PersistDom('${esc(s.id)}','qr')" ${editable?'':'disabled'}></div><div><label class="label">出庫先</label><input id="v124_dest_${esc(id)}" class="input" value="${esc(entry.destination||'')}" oninput="v124PersistDom('${esc(s.id)}','qr')" ${editable?'':'disabled'}></div></div><label class="label">メモ</label><input id="v124_memo_${esc(id)}" class="input" value="${esc(entry.memo||'')}" oninput="v124PersistDom('${esc(s.id)}','qr')" ${editable?'':'disabled'}></section>`}
function v124SectionHeader(s,section){const x=v124SectionStats(s,section),state=v124SectionStatus(s,section),p=v124SavedPrefix(section),label=state==='confirmed'?'確定済み':state==='submitted'?'入力完了':state==='incomplete'?'下書き保存済み':'入力中';return `<div class="card"><div class="countStatGrid"><div class="countStat"><span class="small">対象</span><b>${x.total}</b></div><div class="countStat"><span class="small">入力済み</span><b>${x.entered}</b></div><div class="countStat"><span class="small">未入力</span><b>${x.missing}</b></div><div class="countStat"><span class="small">状態</span><b>${label}</b></div></div><div class="small" style="margin-top:7px">保存：${esc(s[`${p}SavedAt`] ? dt(s[`${p}SavedAt`]):'－')} / 入力者：${esc(s[`${p}SavedBy`]||'－')} / 提出：${esc(s[`${p}SubmittedAt`] ? dt(s[`${p}SubmittedAt`]):'－')} / 確定：${esc(s[`${p}ConfirmedAt`] ? dt(s[`${p}ConfirmedAt`]):'－')}</div></div>`}
function v124SectionPage(id,section,msg='',filter=false){const s=countSession(id);if(!s)return inventoryCountHome('棚卸しが見つかりません。');v124NormalizeSession(s);if(section==='kataoka'&&(s.room!=='room2'||!['submitted','confirmed'].includes(s.normalStatus)))return inventoryCountHome('片岡在庫は第2冷蔵庫の通常在庫を提出後に入力できます。');setScreen('v124SectionPage',[id,section,msg,filter]);const editable=v124Editable(s,section),missing=v124Missing(s,section),entries=v124EntryMap(s,section),all=v124SectionRows(s,section),lines=filter?all.filter(l=>missing.some(x=>v124LineId(x,section)===v124LineId(l,section))):all;const content=section==='qr'?lines.map(l=>v124QrLineHtml(s,l,editable,entries.get(v124LineId(l,'qr'))||{id:v124LineId(l,'qr')})).join(''):lines.map(l=>v124NormalLineHtml(s,l,section,editable,entries.get(v124LineId(l,section))||{id:v124LineId(l,section),weightEntries:[{id:'w-1',packKg:'',caseCount:''}]})).join('');pageShell(`${v124SectionLabel(section)} 棚卸し入力`,`${esc(s.countNo)} / ${esc(s.roomLabel)}`,`${v123Test(s)?v120TestBadge():''}${okHtml(msg)}${v124SectionHeader(s,section)}${section==='normal'&&v123CanPrintSheet(s)?`<div class="card notice"><b>入力前・途中用</b><button class="btn btn2" style="width:100%;margin-top:7px" onclick="v124PrintSheet('${esc(id)}')">通常在庫の記入票を印刷</button></div>`:''}<div class="card"><div class="grid"><button class="btn btn2" onclick="v124SectionPage('${esc(id)}','${section}','',${!filter})">${filter?'全件を表示':'未入力だけ表示'}</button><button class="btn btn2" onclick="v124ShowMissing('${esc(id)}','${section}')">未入力一覧（${missing.length}件）</button></div></div><div class="card"><b>${v124SectionLabel(section)}</b><div class="small">空欄は未入力、0は確認済みの在庫なしです。画面下でまとめて確認・保存します。</div>${content||'<div class="small">対象品目はありません。</div>'}</div>${editable?`<div class="card"><button class="btn green" style="width:100%" onclick="v124ReviewEntries('${esc(id)}','${section}')">入力内容をまとめて確認</button></div>`:''}`)}
function v124ShowMissing(id,section){const s=countSession(id),rows=v124Missing(s,section);if(!rows.length)return alert('未入力はありません。');if(confirm(`${v124SectionLabel(section)}の未入力 ${rows.length}件\n\n${rows.map(x=>x.itemName||'品名').join('\n')}\n\nOKで未入力だけを表示します。`))v124SectionPage(id,section,'未入力項目を赤枠で表示しています。',true)}
function v124ReviewEntries(id,section){v124PersistDom(id,section);const s=countSession(id),missing=v124Missing(s,section),entries=v124DraftEntries(s,section);v124ApplyEntries(s,section,entries);const sum=v124SectionRows(s,section).map(x=>`<li>${esc(x.itemName||'品名')}：${v123Entered(x)?esc(fmt(x.actualQty)+(x.unit||'')):'<b style="color:#b91c1c">未入力</b>'}</li>`).join('');pageShell(`${v124SectionLabel(section)} 入力内容の確認`,`${esc(s.countNo)} / 未入力 ${missing.length}件`,`${missing.length?`<div class="card notice"><b>未入力が${missing.length}件あります。</b><div class="small">入力済みの値は消えません。下書き保存はできますが提出はできません。</div></div>`:'<div class="card ok"><b>全件入力済みです。入力完了として提出できます。</b></div>'}<div class="card"><ul>${sum}</ul></div><div class="grid"><button class="btn blue" onclick="v124SaveSection('${esc(id)}','${section}','draft')">${missing.length?'未入力のまま下書き保存':'下書き保存'}</button>${!missing.length?`<button class="btn green" onclick="v124SaveSection('${esc(id)}','${section}','submit')">入力完了として提出</button>`:''}<button class="btn btn2" onclick="v124SectionPage('${esc(id)}','${section}')">入力画面へ戻る</button></div>`)}
async function v124SaveSection(id,section,action){const s=countSession(id);v124PersistDom(id,section);const missing=v124Missing(s,section),entries=v124DraftEntries(s,section);if(action==='submit'&&missing.length)return v124ReviewEntries(id,section);const op=crypto?.randomUUID?.()||`${Date.now()}-${Math.random()}`;v161ShowProcessing(`${v124SectionLabel(section)}を保存しています`);try{const ans=await callRpc('v124_save_inventory_count_section',{p_id:STATE_ID,p_session_id:id,p_section:section,p_expected_section_revision:Number(s[v124RevisionKey(section)])||0,p_entries:entries,p_action:action,p_operation_id:op});if(ans?.conflict)throw new Error(ans.message||'別端末で更新されています。');if(!ans?.ok)throw new Error(ans?.message||'保存できませんでした。');v124ClearDraft(id,section);await loadCloud();inventoryCountHome(`${v124SectionLabel(section)}を${action==='submit'?'入力完了として提出':'下書き保存'}しました。`)}catch(e){alert(`${v124SectionLabel(section)}を保存できませんでした。\n${v161ErrorText(e)}\n\n入力内容はこの端末に保持しています。`);v124SectionPage(id,section)}finally{v161HideProcessing()}}
async function v124ReturnSection(id,section){const s=countSession(id);if(!canAdmin())return alert('差戻しできるのは管理者のみです。');if(!confirm(`${v124SectionLabel(section)}を入力へ差戻しますか？`))return;try{const ans=await callRpc('v124_return_inventory_count_section',{p_id:STATE_ID,p_session_id:id,p_section:section,p_expected_section_revision:Number(s[v124RevisionKey(section)])||0,p_operation_id:crypto?.randomUUID?.()||`${Date.now()}-${Math.random()}`});if(!ans?.ok)throw new Error(ans?.message||'差戻しできませんでした。');await loadCloud();inventoryCountHome(`${v124SectionLabel(section)}を差戻しました。`)}catch(e){alert(v161ErrorText(e))}}
async function v124ConfirmSection(id,section){let s=countSession(id);if(!canAdmin()||!s||v124SectionStatus(s,section)!=='submitted')return alert('管理者が提出済み工程だけ確定できます。');if(!confirm(`${v124SectionLabel(section)}を確定します。${v123Test(s)?'テストのため在庫には反映しません。':'確定済み工程だけを反映します。'} よろしいですか？`))return;v161ShowProcessing(`${v124SectionLabel(section)}を確定しています`);try{await loadCloud();s=countSession(id);if(!s||v124SectionStatus(s,section)!=='submitted')throw new Error('工程の状態が更新されています。');if(section==='qr'&&!v123Test(s)){const qr=await callRpc('confirm_qr_inventory_session',{p_session_id:s.id,p_operation_id:`v124-qr-${s.id}`,p_room:s.room,p_entries:(s.qrLines||[]).map(qrCountEntry),p_user_id:db.userId||null,p_user_name:db.user||null,p_device_meta:{deviceId:deviceId(),userAgent:navigator.userAgent||''},p_operation_meta:operationMeta('v124_qr_inventory_count',{sessionId:s.id,countNo:s.countNo})});if(!qr?.ok)throw new Error(qr?.message||'QR在庫確定に失敗しました。')}const ans=await callRpc('v124_confirm_inventory_count_section',{p_id:STATE_ID,p_session_id:id,p_section:section,p_expected_section_revision:Number(s[v124RevisionKey(section)])||0,p_operation_id:crypto?.randomUUID?.()||`${Date.now()}-${Math.random()}`});if(ans?.conflict)throw new Error(ans.message||'別端末で更新されています。');if(!ans?.ok)throw new Error(ans?.message||'確定できませんでした。');await loadCloud();inventoryCountHome(`${v124SectionLabel(section)}を確定しました。`)}catch(e){alert(`${v124SectionLabel(section)}を確定できませんでした。\n${v161ErrorText(e)}`)}finally{v161HideProcessing()}}
function v124PriceRows(s){return ['normal','qr','kataoka'].flatMap(section=>v124Sections(s).includes(section)&&v124SectionStatus(s,section)==='confirmed'?v124SectionRows(s,section).map(line=>({line,isQr:section==='qr',section})):[])}
function v124LineKg(line,isQr=false){if(v123Entered(line)&&Number(line.actualQty)===0)return 0;return v119LineActualKg(line,isQr)}
function v124PricesComplete(s){const rows=v124PriceRows(s);return rows.length>0&&rows.every(({line,isQr})=>Number(line.actualQty)===0||(v124LineKg(line,isQr)>0&&v119StrictDecimal(line.unitPriceYenPerKg)!==null))}
function v124PricePage(id,msg=''){const s=countSession(id);if(!s||!v119CanPriceInput())return inventoryCountHome();const rows=v124PriceRows(s);if(!rows.length)return inventoryCountHome('確定済みの棚卸し工程がありません。');setScreen('v124PricePage',[id,msg]);pageShell('棚卸し単価入力',`${esc(s.countNo)} / 確定済み工程だけが対象`,`${okHtml(msg)}<div class="card notice"><b>実数0の行は単価を空欄のまま保存できます。</b><div class="small">Enterで次の単価欄、Shift＋Enterで前の単価欄へ移動します。最後は保存ボタンへ移動します。</div></div><div class="card"><div class="weeklyWrap"><table class="table"><thead><tr><th>区分</th><th>品名・ロット</th><th>棚卸kg</th><th>単価（円/kg）</th><th>金額</th></tr></thead><tbody>${rows.map(({line,isQr,section},i)=>{const kg=v124LineKg(line,isQr),zero=Number(line.actualQty)===0,price=v119StrictDecimal(line.unitPriceYenPerKg),amount=zero?0:(kg&&price!==null?Math.round(kg*price):'');return `<tr><td>${esc(section==='kataoka'?'片岡在庫':isQr?'QR':line.type==='special'?'その他':'通常')}</td><td>${esc(line.itemName||'')}${isQr?`<br><small>${esc(line.lotNo||line.qrKey||'')}</small>`:''}</td><td>${kg===null?'－':fmt(kg)}</td><td><input class="input v124PriceInput" data-v124-price-index="${i}" data-v124-price-needed="${zero?'false':'true'}" id="v124price_${i}" inputmode="decimal" value="${zero?'':esc(price??'')}" ${zero?'disabled':''}></td><td id="v124amount_${i}">${amount===''?'－':fmt(amount)}</td></tr>`}).join('')}</tbody></table></div><button id="v124PriceSave" class="btn green" style="width:100%;margin-top:14px" onclick="v124SavePrices('${esc(id)}')">単価を保存</button></div>`);document.querySelectorAll('.v124PriceInput').forEach(input=>input.addEventListener('keydown',v124PriceKeydown))}
function v124PriceKeydown(event){if(event.key!=='Enter'||event.isComposing||event.keyCode===229)return;event.preventDefault();const list=[...document.querySelectorAll('.v124PriceInput')].filter(x=>!x.disabled&&x.offsetParent!==null&&x.dataset.v124PriceNeeded==='true');const current=list.indexOf(event.currentTarget),next=list[current+(event.shiftKey?-1:1)];if(next){next.focus();next.select?.()}else if(!event.shiftKey)document.getElementById('v124PriceSave')?.focus();else list.at(-1)?.focus()}
async function v124SavePrices(id){const s=countSession(id),rows=v124PriceRows(s),prices=[],bad=[];rows.forEach(({line,isQr},i)=>{const zero=Number(line.actualQty)===0,price=zero?null:v119StrictDecimal(document.getElementById(`v124price_${i}`)?.value),kg=v124LineKg(line,isQr);if(!zero&&(kg===null||kg<=0||price===null||price<0))bad.push(line.itemName||'品名');prices.push({lineId:isQr?null:String(line.lineId||''),qrKey:isQr?String(line.qrKey||line.qr_key||''):null,unitPriceYenPerKg:price})});if(bad.length)return alert(`単価またはkg換算を確認してください。\n${bad.join('\n')}`);if(!confirm('確定済み工程の単価と金額を保存します。よろしいですか？'))return;v161ShowProcessing('単価を保存しています');try{const ans=await callRpc('v124_save_inventory_count_prices',{p_id:STATE_ID,p_expected_version:stateVersion,p_session_id:id,p_prices:prices});if(!ans?.ok)throw new Error(ans?.message||'単価を保存できませんでした。');await loadCloud();inventoryCountHome('単価を保存しました。')}catch(e){alert(v161ErrorText(e))}finally{v161HideProcessing()}}
function v124ExcelRows(s,withPrice){return v124PriceRows(s).map(({line,isQr,section})=>({group:section==='kataoka'?'片岡在庫':isQr?'QR':line.type==='special'?'その他':'通常',item:line.itemName||'',lot:isQr?(line.lotNo||line.qrKey||''):'',supplier:line.supplier||line.supplier_name||'',container:isQr?(line.containerType||line.unit||''):(line.unit||'kg'),weightBreakdown:isQr?'':v123WeightBreakdown(line),packKg:isQr?(line.packWeight||''):(line.packKg??''),cases:line.caseCount??'',remainderKg:line.remainderKg??'',actualKg:v124LineKg(line,isQr),systemQty:line.systemQty??'',actualQty:line.actualQty??'',diff:line.actualQty===null||line.actualQty===undefined?'':Number(line.actualQty)-Number(line.systemQty||0),unitPrice:withPrice?(Number(line.actualQty)===0?'':v119StrictDecimal(line.unitPriceYenPerKg)): '',amount:withPrice?(Number(line.actualQty)===0?0:(line.amountYen??(v124LineKg(line,isQr)!==null&&v119StrictDecimal(line.unitPriceYenPerKg)!==null?Math.round(v124LineKg(line,isQr)*v119StrictDecimal(line.unitPriceYenPerKg)) :''))):'',memo:line.memo||''}))}
function v124ExcelCell(x){return typeof x==='number'&&Number.isFinite(x)?x:inventoryCountExcelSafeText(x)}
function v124Sheet(ws,rows,session,withPrice,isTotal=false){const heads=isTotal?['冷蔵庫','区分','品名','ロット','仕入先','荷姿','数量内訳','量目kg','ケース数','端数kg','棚卸kg','開始時帳簿','棚卸し実数','差異','単価（円/kg）','金額（円）','メモ']:['区分','品名','ロット','仕入先','荷姿','数量内訳','量目kg','ケース数','端数kg','棚卸kg','開始時帳簿','棚卸し実数','差異','単価（円/kg）','金額（円）','メモ'];const stage=withPrice?'単価・金額確定版':'数量確定版（単価未入力）',title=`${v123Test(session)?'【TEST】':''}棚卸し結果（${stage}） ${isTotal?'全冷蔵庫':session.roomLabel} ${session.countNo}`;const meta=[['棚卸し番号',session.countNo],['状態',stage],['冷蔵庫',isTotal?'第2・第3冷蔵庫':session.roomLabel],['出力日時',dt(nowIso())],['出力段階',withPrice?'単価・金額確定':'数量確定'],...(session.room==='room2'&&v124SectionStatus(session,'kataoka')!=='confirmed'&&!isTotal?[['片岡在庫','未確認（集計対象外）']]:[])];const values=rows.map(r=>{const a=[r.group,r.item,r.lot,r.supplier,r.container,r.weightBreakdown,r.packKg,r.cases,r.remainderKg,r.actualKg,r.systemQty,r.actualQty,r.diff,r.unitPrice,r.amount,r.memo].map(v124ExcelCell);return isTotal?[r.roomLabel,...a]:a});const body=[[title],[],...meta,[],heads,...values];const totalRow=meta.length+4+values.length+1;body.push([isTotal?'':'合計',...(Array(heads.length-1).fill(''))]);XLSX.utils.sheet_add_aoa(ws,body,{origin:'A1'});const actualCol=isTotal?13:12,amountCol=isTotal?16:15;ws[XLSX.utils.encode_cell({r:totalRow-1,c:actualCol-1})]={t:'n',f:`SUM(${XLSX.utils.encode_col(actualCol-1)}${meta.length+5}:${XLSX.utils.encode_col(actualCol-1)}${totalRow-1})`};if(withPrice)ws[XLSX.utils.encode_cell({r:totalRow-1,c:amountCol-1})]={t:'n',f:`SUM(${XLSX.utils.encode_col(amountCol-1)}${meta.length+5}:${XLSX.utils.encode_col(amountCol-1)}${totalRow-1})`};ws['!merges']=[{s:{r:0,c:0},e:{r:0,c:heads.length-1}}];ws['!autofilter']={ref:XLSX.utils.encode_range({s:{r:meta.length+3,c:0},e:{r:totalRow-2,c:heads.length-1}})};ws['!cols']=(isTotal?[12]:[]).concat([10,22,18,18,15,34,10,10,10,12,13,13,11,14,14,36]).map(wch=>({wch}));inventoryCountExcelStyle(ws,'A1',{font:{name:'Yu Gothic',sz:15,bold:true,color:{rgb:'FFFFFF'}},fill:{patternType:'solid',fgColor:{rgb:withPrice?'166534':'1D4ED8'}}})}
function v124SessionDate(s){try{return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('sv-SE',{timeZone:'Asia/Tokyo'}).format(new Date(s.startedAt))}catch{return String(s.startedAt||'').slice(0,10)}}
function v124BundleCandidates(seed){return countSessions().filter(s=>s.id!==seed.id&&s.room!==seed.room&&v123Test(s)===v123Test(seed)&&v124SessionDate(s)===v124SessionDate(seed)&&v124PriceRows(s).length).sort((a,b)=>new Date(b.startedAt)-new Date(a.startedAt))}
async function v124DownloadExcel(id,otherId=''){const s=countSession(id);if(!s||!v124PriceRows(s).length)return alert('通常またはQRの少なくとも1工程を確定してからExcelを出力してください。');const withPrice=v124PricesComplete(s),other=otherId?countSession(otherId):null,all=[s,...(other&&v124PriceRows(other).length?[other]:[])];if(!ensureXlsxReady())return;const wb=XLSX.utils.book_new();if(all.length===2){const combined=all.flatMap(x=>v124ExcelRows(x,withPrice&&v124PricesComplete(x)).map(r=>({...r,roomLabel:x.roomLabel})));const w=XLSX.utils.aoa_to_sheet([]);v124Sheet(w,combined,s,withPrice&&all.every(v124PricesComplete),true);XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb,w,'全冷蔵庫合計');for(const room of ['room2','room3']){const x=all.find(z=>z.room===room);if(x){const ws=XLSX.utils.aoa_to_sheet([]);v124Sheet(ws,v124ExcelRows(x,withPrice&&v124PricesComplete(x)),x,withPrice&&v124PricesComplete(x));XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb,ws,room==='room2'?'第2冷蔵庫':'第3冷蔵庫')}}}else{const ws=XLSX.utils.aoa_to_sheet([]);v124Sheet(ws,v124ExcelRows(s,withPrice),s,withPrice);XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb,ws,safeSheetName(s.roomLabel||'棚卸し'))}const stage=withPrice?'単価金額確定版':'数量確定版',file=`${v123Test(s)?'TEST_':''}棚卸し結果_${stage}_${all.map(x=>x.roomLabel).join('・')}_${s.countNo}.xlsx`.replace(/[\\/:*?"<>|]/g,'_');downloadWorkbookXlsx(wb,file);try{await callRpc('v124_mark_inventory_count_excel_export',{p_id:STATE_ID,p_expected_version:stateVersion,p_session_ids:all.map(x=>x.id),p_file_name:file,p_stage:withPrice?'priced':'quantity'});await loadCloud();inventoryCountHome('Excelを出力し、出力記録を保存しました。')}catch(e){alert(`Excelは出力しましたが、出力記録を保存できませんでした。\n${v161ErrorText(e)}`)}}
function v124ExcelButtons(s){const eligible=v124PriceRows(s).length>0;if(!eligible)return '';const candidates=v124BundleCandidates(s),choose=candidates.length?`<button class="btn blue" onclick="v124ChooseBundle('${esc(s.id)}')">第2・第3を統合してExcel</button>`:'';return `<button class="btn blue" onclick="v124DownloadExcel('${esc(s.id)}')">${v124PricesComplete(s)?'単価・金額確定版Excel':'数量確定版Excel'}</button>${choose}`}
function v124ChooseBundle(id){const s=countSession(id),c=v124BundleCandidates(s);if(!c.length)return v124DownloadExcel(id);const message=c.map((x,i)=>`${i+1}. ${x.roomLabel} / ${x.countNo} / 開始 ${dt(x.startedAt)}`).join('\n');const n=Number(prompt(`同日・同モードのもう一方の冷蔵庫を選択してください。\n${message}`, '1'));const target=c[n-1];if(!target)return alert('選択が不正です。');v124DownloadExcel(id,target.id)}

class="mini" style="margin-top:8px">${v124ExcelButtons(s)}${!['confirmed','test_confirmed'].includes(s.status)&&canAdmin()?`<button class="btn red" /* New sessions are split before the dedicated create RPC; old sessions normalize lazily without loss. */
const v124StartCore=v123StartSession;v123StartSession=async function(room,isTest=false){const r=await v124StartCore(room,isTest);v124AllSessions();return r};
const v124NewSessionCore=v123NewSession;v123NewSession=function(room,isTest=false){return v124NewSessionCore(room,isTest).then(s=>v124NormalizeSession(s))};
inventoryCountHome=v124Home;inventoryCountPage=(id,msg='')=>v124SectionPage(id,'normal',msg);downloadInventoryCountExcel=v124DownloadExcel;v119InventoryCountPricePage=v124PricePage;v119SaveInventoryCountPrices=v124SavePrices;printInventoryCount=(id,mode)=>mode==='sheet'?v124PrintSheet(id):v123PrintDiff(id);
/* v1.124 confirms only through v124 RPC. Never re-enable generic inventoryCounts writes. */
confirmInventoryCount=(id)=>v124ConfirmSection(id,'normal');


/* v1.124 production hardening: all inventory count mutations remain dedicated-RPC only. */
function v124NormalizeSession(s){
 if(!s)return s; const legacy=(s.lines||[]).filter(v124IsKataoka), normal=(s.lines||[]).filter(x=>!v124IsKataoka), seen=new Set(), kataoka=[];
 for(const line of [...(Array.isArray(s.kataokaLines)?s.kataokaLines:[]),...legacy]){const key=String(line?.lineId||line?.id||'');if(!key||seen.has(key))continue;seen.add(key);kataoka.push(line)}
 s.lines=normal;if(s.room==='room2'){s.kataokaLines=kataoka;s.kataokaStatus=s.kataokaStatus||(s.status==='confirmed'||s.status==='test_confirmed'?'confirmed':'counting');s.kataokaRevision=Math.max(0,Number(s.kataokaRevision??s.revision??0));s.kataokaLines.forEach(l=>{v119EnsureCountLine(l);v123NormalizeWeightEntries(l)})}else{ s.kataokaLines=[];delete s.kataokaStatus;delete s.kataokaRevision }
 s.countSchemaVersion=124;return v124DeriveStatus(s)
}
function v124AddWeightEntry(id,lineId,section){
 const s=countSession(id);if(!s||!v124Editable(s,section))return;v124PersistDom(id,section);const map=v124EntryMap(s,section),e=map.get(String(lineId));if(!e)return;if((e.weightEntries||[]).length>=20)return alert('量目明細は20行までです。');e.weightEntries=e.weightEntries||[];e.weightEntries.push({id:`w-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,7)}`,packKg:'',caseCount:''});map.set(String(lineId),e);v124WriteDraft(id,section,[...map.values()]);v124SectionPage(id,section)
}
function v124RemoveWeightEntry(id,lineId,section,entryId){
 const s=countSession(id);if(!s||!v124Editable(s,section))return;v124PersistDom(id,section);const map=v124EntryMap(s,section),e=map.get(String(lineId));if(!e)return;e.weightEntries=(e.weightEntries||[]).filter(x=>String(x.id)!==String(entryId));if(!e.weightEntries.length)e.weightEntries=[{id:'w-1',packKg:'',caseCount:''}];map.set(String(lineId),e);v124WriteDraft(id,section,[...map.values()]);v124SectionPage(id,section)
}
function v124NormalLineHtml(s,line,section,editable,entry){
 const id=v124LineId(line,section),kg=v119IsKgLine(line),actual=kg?v124WeightTotal(entry.weightEntries,entry.remainderKg):v119StrictDecimal(entry.actualQty),missing=actual===null,diff=missing?null:Math.round((actual-Number(line.systemQty||0))*1000)/1000;
 const rows=(entry.weightEntries||[{id:'w-1',packKg:'',caseCount:''}]).map((e,n)=>`<div class="row" data-v124-weight="${esc(id)}" data-entry-id="${esc(e.id)}" style="margin-top:6px"><div><label class="label">量目kg ${n+1}</label><input id="v124_pack_${esc(id)}_${esc(e.id)}" class="input" inputmode="decimal" value="${esc(e.packKg??'')}" oninput="v124UpdateWeightTotal('${esc(s.id)}','${esc(id)}','${section}')" ${editable?'':'disabled'}></div><div><label class="label">ケース数</label><input id="v124_case_${esc(id)}_${esc(e.id)}" class="input" inputmode="decimal" value="${esc(e.caseCount??'')}" oninput="v124UpdateWeightTotal('${esc(s.id)}','${esc(id)}','${section}')" ${editable?'':'disabled'}></div>${editable&&n>0?`<div style="align-self:end"><button class="btn btn2" type="button" onclick="v124RemoveWeightEntry('${esc(s.id)}','${esc(id)}','${section}','${esc(e.id)}')">削除</button></div>`:''}</div>`).join('');
 const weightInput=kg?`${rows}${editable?`<button class="btn btn2" type="button" style="margin-top:8px" onclick="v124AddWeightEntry('${esc(s.id)}','${esc(id)}','${section}')">＋ 量目を追加</button>`:''}<div class="row" style="margin-top:7px"><div><label class="label">端数kg</label><input id="v124_rem_${esc(id)}" class="input" inputmode="decimal" value="${esc(entry.remainderKg??'')}" oninput="v124UpdateWeightTotal('${esc(s.id)}','${esc(id)}','${section}')" ${editable?'':'disabled'}></div><div><label class="label">合計実数</label><div class="input" id="v124_total_${esc(id)}" style="background:#f8fafc">${missing?'未入力':fmt(actual)+' kg'}</div></div></div>`:`<div class="row" style="margin-top:7px"><div><label class="label">実数（${esc(line.unit||'単位')}）</label><input id="v124_qty_${esc(id)}" class="input" inputmode="decimal" value="${esc(entry.actualQty??'')}" placeholder="空欄=未入力 / 0=在庫なし" oninput="v124PersistDom('${esc(s.id)}','${section}')" ${editable?'':'disabled'}></div><div><label class="label">1単位あたりkg</label><input id="v124_kgper_${esc(id)}" class="input" inputmode="decimal" value="${esc(entry.kgPerUnit??'')}" oninput="v124PersistDom('${esc(s.id)}','${section}')" ${editable?'':'disabled'}></div></div>`;
 return `<section class="card countLine ${missing?'v123Missing':''}"><div class="countMeta"><div><b>${esc(line.itemName)}</b><div class="small">${esc(line.group||'')} / 開始時帳簿：${fmt(line.systemQty)} ${esc(kg?'kg':line.unit||'単位')}</div></div><span class="badge ${missing?'unset':diff===0?'normal':diff<0?'shortage':'excess'}">${missing?'未入力':`${diff>0?'+':''}${fmt(diff)} ${esc(kg?'kg':line.unit||'')}`}</span></div>${weightInput}<div class="row" style="margin-top:7px"><div><label class="label">仕入先（必要時）</label><input id="v124_supplier_${esc(id)}" class="input" value="${esc(entry.supplier||'')}" oninput="v124PersistDom('${esc(s.id)}','${section}')" ${editable?'':'disabled'}></div><div><label class="label">仕入先を必須にする</label><label class="checkRow"><input id="v124_required_${esc(id)}" type="checkbox" ${entry.supplierRequired?'checked':''} onchange="v124PersistDom('${esc(s.id)}','${section}')" ${editable?'':'disabled'}> 要確認</label></div></div><label class="label" style="margin-top:7px">メモ</label><input id="v124_memo_${esc(id)}" class="input" value="${esc(entry.memo||'')}" oninput="v124PersistDom('${esc(s.id)}','${section}')" ${editable?'':'disabled'}></section>`
}
const V124_PRICE_DRAFT_PREFIX='seika_inventory_count_price_draft_v124';
function v124PriceDraftKey(id){return `${V124_PRICE_DRAFT_PREFIX}:${db?.userId||'anonymous'}:${id}`}
function v124PriceDraft(id){try{return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(v124PriceDraftKey(id))||'{}')}catch{return {}}}
function v124PriceDraftSet(id,index,value){const x=v124PriceDraft(id);x[index]=value;try{localStorage.setItem(v124PriceDraftKey(id),JSON.stringify(x))}catch{} const amount=document.getElementById(`v124amount_${index}`),input=document.getElementById(`v124price_${index}`);if(amount&&input){const kg=Number(input.dataset.v124Kg||0),p=v119StrictDecimal(value);amount.textContent=p===null?'－':fmt(Math.round(kg*p))}}
function v124SessionFullyPriced(s){return v124Sections(s).every(x=>v124SectionStatus(s,x)==='confirmed')&&s.priceStatus==='priced'&&v124PricesComplete(s)}
function v124PricePage(id,msg=''){
 const s=countSession(id);if(!s||!v119CanPriceInput())return inventoryCountHome();const rows=v124PriceRows(s);if(!rows.length)return inventoryCountHome('確定済みの棚卸し工程がありません。');const draft=v124PriceDraft(id);setScreen('v124PricePage',[id,msg]);pageShell('棚卸し単価入力',`${esc(s.countNo)} / 確定済み工程だけが対象`,`${okHtml(msg)}<div class="card notice"><b>実数0の行は単価を空欄のまま保存できます。</b><div class="small">Enterで次の単価欄、Shift＋Enterで前の単価欄へ移動します。最後は保存ボタンへ移動します。入力値は端末にも保持します。</div></div><div class="card"><div class="weeklyWrap"><table class="table"><thead><tr><th>区分</th><th>品名・ロット</th><th>棚卸kg</th><th>単価（円/kg）</th><th>金額</th></tr></thead><tbody>${rows.map(({line,isQr,section},i)=>{const kg=v124LineKg(line,isQr),zero=Number(line.actualQty)===0,price=Object.hasOwn(draft,i)?v119StrictDecimal(draft[i]):v119StrictDecimal(line.unitPriceYenPerKg),amount=zero?0:(kg&&price!==null?Math.round(kg*price):'');return `<tr><td>${esc(section==='kataoka'?'片岡在庫':isQr?'QR':line.type==='special'?'その他':'通常')}</td><td>${esc(line.itemName||'')}${isQr?`<br><small>${esc(line.lotNo||line.qrKey||'')}</small>`:''}</td><td>${kg===null?'－':fmt(kg)}</td><td><input class="input v124PriceInput" data-v124-price-index="${i}" data-v124-price-needed="${zero?'false':'true'}" data-v124-kg="${esc(kg??'')}" id="v124price_${i}" inputmode="decimal" value="${zero?'':esc(price??'')}" oninput="v124PriceDraftSet('${esc(id)}',${i},this.value)" ${zero?'disabled':''}></td><td id="v124amount_${i}">${amount===''?'－':fmt(amount)}</td></tr>`}).join('')}</tbody></table></div><button id="v124PriceSave" class="btn green" style="width:100%;margin-top:14px" onclick="v124SavePrices('${esc(id)}')">単価を保存</button></div>`);document.querySelectorAll('.v124PriceInput').forEach(input=>input.addEventListener('keydown',v124PriceKeydown))
}
function v124PriceKeydown(event){if(event.key!=='Enter'||event.isComposing||event.keyCode===229)return;event.preventDefault();const list=[...document.querySelectorAll('.v124PriceInput')].filter(x=>!x.disabled&&x.offsetParent!==null&&x.dataset.v124PriceNeeded==='true'),i=list.indexOf(event.currentTarget),next=list[i+(event.shiftKey?-1:1)];if(next){next.focus();next.select?.()}else if(!event.shiftKey)document.getElementById('v124PriceSave')?.focus()}
async function v124SavePrices(id){const s=countSession(id),rows=v124PriceRows(s),prices=[],bad=[];rows.forEach(({line,isQr},i)=>{const zero=Number(line.actualQty)===0,price=zero?null:v119StrictDecimal(document.getElementById(`v124price_${i}`)?.value),kg=v124LineKg(line,isQr);if(!zero&&(kg===null||kg<=0||price===null||price<0))bad.push(line.itemName||'品名');prices.push({lineId:isQr?null:String(line.lineId||''),qrKey:isQr?String(line.qrKey||line.qr_key||''):null,unitPriceYenPerKg:price})});if(bad.length)return alert(`単価またはkg換算を確認してください。\n${bad.join('\n')}`);if(!confirm('確定済み工程の単価と金額を保存します。よろしいですか？'))return;v161ShowProcessing('単価を保存しています');try{const ans=await callRpc('v124_save_inventory_count_prices',{p_id:STATE_ID,p_expected_version:stateVersion,p_session_id:id,p_prices:prices});if(!ans?.ok)throw new Error(ans?.message||'単価を保存できませんでした。');try{localStorage.removeItem(v124PriceDraftKey(id))}catch{}await loadCloud();inventoryCountHome('単価を保存しました。')}catch(e){alert(v161ErrorText(e))}finally{v161HideProcessing()}}
async function v124DownloadExcel(id,otherId=''){
 const s=countSession(id),other=otherId?countSession(otherId):null,all=[s,...(other&&v124PriceRows(other).length?[other]:[])].filter(Boolean);if(!s||!v124PriceRows(s).length)return alert('確定済み工程がある棚卸しだけExcelを出力できます。');if(!ensureXlsxReady())return;const allPriced=all.every(v124SessionFullyPriced),wb=XLSX.utils.book_new();
 if(all.length===2){const combined=all.flatMap(x=>v124ExcelRows(x,allPriced).map(r=>({...r,roomLabel:x.roomLabel}))),w=XLSX.utils.aoa_to_sheet([]);v124Sheet(w,combined,s,allPriced,true);XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb,w,'全冷蔵庫合計');for(const room of ['room2','room3']){const x=all.find(z=>z.room===room),ws=XLSX.utils.aoa_to_sheet([]);v124Sheet(ws,v124ExcelRows(x,allPriced),x,allPriced);XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb,ws,room==='room2'?'第2冷蔵庫':'第3冷蔵庫')}}else{const ws=XLSX.utils.aoa_to_sheet([]);v124Sheet(ws,v124ExcelRows(s,allPriced),s,allPriced);XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb,ws,safeSheetName(s.roomLabel||'棚卸し'))}
 const stage=allPriced?'単価金額確定版':'数量確定版',file=`${v123Test(s)?'TEST_':''}棚卸し結果_${stage}_${all.map(x=>x.roomLabel).join('・')}_${s.countNo}.xlsx`.replace(/[\\/:*?"<>|]/g,'_');downloadWorkbookXlsx(wb,file);try{const ans=await callRpc('v124_mark_inventory_count_excel_export',{p_id:STATE_ID,p_expected_version:stateVersion,p_session_ids:all.map(x=>x.id),p_file_name:file,p_stage:allPriced?'priced':'quantity'});if(!ans?.ok)throw new Error(ans?.message||'出力記録を保存できませんでした。');await loadCloud();inventoryCountHome('Excelを出力し、出力記録を保存しました。')}catch(e){alert(`Excelは出力しましたが、出力記録を保存できませんでした。\n${v161ErrorText(e)}`)}}
function v124CanDocument(session){return Boolean(canAdmin()&&session&&session.status==='confirmed'&&!session.reversedAt&&v124SessionFullyPriced(session)&&session.excelExportedAt)}
function v124CanPurge(session){return Boolean(v124CanDocument(session)&&session.documentSavedAt&&!session.detailsDeletedAt&&v119CalendarMonthsPassed(session.confirmedAt,3))}
async function v124DeleteInventoryCountDetails(id){if(!canAdmin())return alert('明細を削除できるのは管理者のみです。');if(!confirm('保存済みExcelと文書管理を確認済みの棚卸し明細（通常・QR・片岡）をサーバー側で削除します。セッション本体と監査記録は残ります。実行しますか？'))return;v161ShowProcessing('棚卸し明細を安全に削除しています');try{const ans=await callRpc('v124_purge_inventory_count_details',{p_id:STATE_ID,p_expected_version:stateVersion,p_session_id:id});if(!ans?.ok)throw new Error(ans?.message||'明細を削除できませんでした');await loadCloud();inventoryCountHome('棚卸し明細を削除しました。セッション本体と監査記録は残っています。')}catch(e){alert(`棚卸し明細を削除できませんでした。\n${v161ErrorText(e)}`)}finally{v161HideProcessing()}}
function v124Home(msg=''){

 const sec=(s,x)=>{const st=v124SectionStats(s,x),status=v124SectionStatus(s,x),open=`v124SectionPage('${esc(s.id)}','${x}')`,show=x!=='kataoka'||(s.room==='room2'&&['submitted','confirmed'].includes(s.normalStatus));if(!show)return '';return `<div class="card"><b>${v124SectionLabel(x)}</b><div class="small">進捗：${st.entered}/${st.total}（未入力 ${st.missing}） / ${esc(status==='confirmed'?'確定済み':status==='submitted'?'入力完了':status==='incomplete'?'下書き保存済み':'入力中')}</div><div class="mini" style="margin-top:8px"><button class="btn ${x==='qr'?'blue':'green'}" onclick="${open}">${x==='kataoka'?'片岡在庫を入力':x==='qr'?'QR在庫を入力':'通常在庫を入力'}</button>${status==='submitted'&&canAdmin()?`<button class="btn orange" onclick="v124ConfirmSection('${esc(s.id)}','${x}')">この工程を確定</button><button class="btn btn2" onclick="v124ReturnSection('${esc(s.id)}','${x}')">差戻し</button>`:''}</div></div>`};

/* ===== v1.126 市場価格持ち越し・片岡在庫明確化 ===== */
function v1126MarketPriceKey(r){return [String(r?.item_name||'').trim(),String(r?.origin||'').trim(),'市場'].join('\u0001')}
function v1126MondayText(value){return v1115Monday(value)}
async function v1126CarryForwardMarketPrices(targetWeek,rows){
 const target=v1126MondayText(targetWeek),source=ymdKey(new Date(new Date(`${target}T00:00:00`).getTime()-7*86400000));
 const sourceRows=(Array.isArray(rows)?rows:[]).filter(r=>v1117PriceType(r)==='market'&&String(r.week_start||'')===source&&Number(r.price_yen_per_kg)>=0);
 if(!sourceRows.length)return alert(`前週 ${source.replaceAll('-','/')} の市場価格は未登録のため、持ち越せません。`);
 const targetRows=(Array.isArray(rows)?rows:[]).filter(r=>v1117PriceType(r)==='market'&&String(r.week_start||'')===target),keys=new Set(targetRows.map(v1126MarketPriceKey)),already=sourceRows.filter(r=>keys.has(v1126MarketPriceKey(r))).length,willInsert=sourceRows.length-already;
 const text=`市場価格を持ち越します。\n\n前週：${source.replaceAll('-','/')}（${sourceRows.length}件）\n対象週：${target.replaceAll('-','/')}\nこの週に既入力：${already}件（0円を含め上書きしません）\n新規候補：${willInsert}件\n\n契約価格は変更しません。実行しますか？`;
 if(!confirm(text))return;
 const button=document.getElementById('v1126CarryMarketBtn');if(button){button.disabled=true;button.textContent='市場価格を持ち越しています…'}
 v161ShowProcessing('市場価格を安全に持ち越しています');
 try{
   const result=await callRpc('carry_forward_market_prices_v1126',{p_source_week:source,p_target_week:target});
   if(!result?.ok)throw new Error(result?.message||'市場価格を持ち越せませんでした。');
   const inserted=Number(result.inserted_count||0),skipped=Number(result.skipped_count||0),count=Number(result.source_count||0);
   await purchasePricesPage(target,`市場価格を持ち越しました。対象 ${count}件 / 新規 ${inserted}件 / 既入力のためスキップ ${skipped}件（0円も保護）`);
 }catch(e){
   alert(`市場価格を持ち越せませんでした。\n${v161ErrorText(e)}`);
   if(button){button.disabled=false;button.textContent='前週の市場価格をこの週へ持ち越す'}
 }finally{v161HideProcessing()}
}

const V128_WEEKLY_KATAOKA_ROOM='kataoka';
const v128WeeklyRoomLabelBase=roomLabel;
roomLabel=function(room){return room===V128_WEEKLY_KATAOKA_ROOM?'片岡冷蔵庫':v128WeeklyRoomLabelBase(room)};
function v128IsWeeklyDisplayRoom(room){return room==='room2'||room==='room3'||room===V128_WEEKLY_KATAOKA_ROOM}
function v128WeeklyDisplayMatch(row,room){
  if(room===V128_WEEKLY_KATAOKA_ROOM)return row.group==='kataoka';
  return row.group!=='kataoka'&&row.room===room;
}
/* Final effective override: display-only Kataoka selector deliberately does not alter coldRoomForSpecialItem(). */
weeklyDisplayRows=function(room,qrStandaloneTotals={}){
  const rows=[];
  allSortedItems().filter(it=>it.active!==false).forEach(it=>rows.push({
    kind:'normal',group:'optimal',room:coldRoomForNormalItem(it),id:it.id,name:it.name,item:it,sort:Number(it.sort||0)
  }));
  specialItems('exchange').forEach(it=>rows.push({
    kind:'special',group:'nonoptimal',tableId:'exchange',room:coldRoomForSpecialItem('exchange',it),id:it.id,name:it.name,item:it,sort:Number(it.sort||0)
  }));
  specialItems('kataoka').forEach(it=>rows.push({
    kind:'special',group:'kataoka',tableId:'kataoka',room:coldRoomForSpecialItem('kataoka',it),id:it.id,name:it.name,item:it,sort:Number(it.sort||0)
  }));
  Object.entries(qrStandaloneTotals||{}).filter(([key,v])=>
    Object.values(v?.days||{}).some(x=>Number(x)>0)||Object.values(v1106WeeklyBreakdown?.largeStandaloneTotals?.[key]?.days||{}).some(x=>Number(x?.bases||0)>0)
  ).forEach(([key,v],i)=>rows.push({kind:'qr_only',group:'qr',room,id:`qr_only_${key}`,qrKey:key,name:String(v.name||key),item:{},sort:100000+i}));
  const groupOrder={optimal:1,nonoptimal:2,kataoka:3,qr:4};
  return rows.filter(row=>!room||v128WeeklyDisplayMatch(row,room)).sort((a,b)=>
    groupOrder[a.group]-groupOrder[b.group]||a.sort-b.sort||String(a.name).localeCompare(String(b.name),'ja')
  );
};
function setWeeklyRoom(room){
  weeklyRoom=v128IsWeeklyDisplayRoom(room)?room:'room2';
  v125WeeklyVisibleWeeks=2;
  return weeklyData();
}
function weeklyRoomTabsHtml(){
  return `<div class="card"><div class="small">表示する冷蔵庫</div><div class="filterBtns"><button class="filterBtn all ${weeklyRoom==='room2'?'on':''}" onclick="setWeeklyRoom('room2')">第2冷蔵庫</button><button class="filterBtn entered ${weeklyRoom==='room3'?'on':''}" onclick="setWeeklyRoom('room3')">第3冷蔵庫</button><button class="filterBtn ${weeklyRoom===V128_WEEKLY_KATAOKA_ROOM?'on':''}" onclick="setWeeklyRoom('kataoka')">片岡冷蔵庫</button></div><div class="small">在庫表は選択した冷蔵庫だけを表示します。片岡冷蔵庫は片岡在庫だけを表示します。</div></div><div class="card"><b>印刷用チェック表</b><div class="small">A4横で、紙の在庫表に近い形で印刷します。未入力欄は手書き用に空欄で出ます。</div><div class="row" style="margin-top:10px"><button class="btn blue" onclick="printInventorySheet('room2plus')">第2冷蔵庫＋片岡在庫を印刷</button><button class="btn green" onclick="printInventorySheet('room3')">第3冷蔵庫を印刷</button></div></div>`;
}
/* Preserve the existing v1.126 Kataoka visual treatment after the later QR renderer override. */
const v128WeeklyTableHtmlCore=weeklyTableHtml;
weeklyTableHtml=function(k,recs,spRecs,open=false,room=weeklyRoom,qrTotals={},qrStandaloneTotals={},qrSpecs={},qrStandaloneSpecs={},qrBreakdown={}){
  let html=v128WeeklyTableHtmlCore(k,recs,spRecs,open,room,qrTotals,qrStandaloneTotals,qrSpecs,qrStandaloneSpecs,qrBreakdown);
  for(const row of weeklyDisplayRows(room,qrStandaloneTotals).filter(row=>row.group==='kataoka')){
    const plain=`<tr><td>${esc(row.name)}</td>`;
    const marked=`<tr class="stockRowKataoka"><td><span class="stockKataokaBadge">片岡在庫</span> ${esc(row.name)}</td>`;
    html=html.replace(plain,marked);
  }
  return html;
};
window.APP_VERSION='v1.128';
document.title='SEIKA＋ v1.128 片岡冷蔵庫表示分離版';

</script>
